### [1] [Valley polarization, Rashba interaction, and weak altermagnetism in inversion-asymmetric MnPS$_\text{3}|$WS$_\text{2}$ van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16454)
*Purba Dutta,Soumajyoti Bid,Nirmal Ganguli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，研究了MnPS₃/WS₂范德华异质结，发现其中存在交错磁性、Rashba自旋劈裂、自旋-谷锁定和谷极化等丰富现象，且电子结构和能带排列可通过外电场和双轴应变有效调控，为自旋电子学和谷电子学应用提供了高度可调的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维材料最新进展的启发，旨在探索通过范德华异质结在反铁磁体系中打破反演对称性，以诱导交错磁性、Rashba效应、自旋-谷锁定和谷极化等新兴现象的可行性。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论的第一性原理计算。

Result: MnPS₃/WS₂异质结具有约1.65 eV的直接带隙半导体行为和I型能带排列；外电场和双轴应变可有效调控电子结构，实现I型与II型能带排列的转变，并能调控交错磁性相和谷劈裂。

Conclusion: 该范德华异质结是一个高度可调的平台，在自旋电子学和谷电子学领域具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: The deliberate breaking of inversion ($\mathcal{P}$) symmetry in antiferromagnets has recently emerged as an effective means to induce various features, such as the emergence of Berry curvature, spin-valley locking, magnetoelectric coupling, and the transition from conventional antiferromagnetism to altermagnetism. Conversely, in non-magnetic systems, inversion symmetry breaking in the presence of strong spin-orbit interaction (SOI) gives rise to momentum-dependent spin splitting via the Rashba effect, enabling tunable spin polarization through external electric fields. Motivated by recent advances in two-dimensional materials, we perform first-principles calculations based on density functional theory to investigate the van der Waals (vdW) heterostructure formed by a $\mathcal{P}$-symmetric MnPS$_3$ monolayer and a WS$_2$ monolayer. We demonstrate that the interface hosts a rich interplay of emergent phenomena, including an altermagnetic phase, Rashba spin splitting, spin-valley locking, and valley polarization. Our results demonstrate that the heterostructure exhibits semiconducting behavior with a direct band gap of approximately 1.65~eV and a type-I band alignment. Remarkably, the electronic structure and band alignment can be effectively tuned between type-I and type-II regimes via an external electric field and in-plane biaxial strain. Furthermore, field-induced modulation enables strong control over the altermagnetic phase and the valley splitting. These findings establish the proposed vdW heterostructure as a highly tunable platform with significant potential for spintronic and valleytronic applications.

</details>


### [2] [Bond reconstruction and vacancy clustering in monolayer silicon carbide from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16483)
*Péter Udvarhelyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用密度泛函理论研究单层碳化硅中单空位及空位聚集体的键重构机制，发现键重构影响缺陷的稳定性、对称性及电子/光学性质，其中碳单空位重构抑制光学活性，而由三个碳空位环绕一个硅空位构成的高稳定聚集体展现出作为红外色心候选材料的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示单层碳化硅中空位相关结构的键重构机制，及其对形成能、对称性、电子和光学性质的影响。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算，结合多种键描述符分析，研究单空位和空位聚集体的面内重构与面外畸变机制。

Result: 孤立单空位存在面内重构和面外畸变共同作用；碳单空位中重构抑制了光学活性；由三个碳空位环绕一个硅空位构成的高稳定聚集体具有三重态基态和良好的Debye-Waller因子，是红外色心候选材料。

Conclusion: 键重构在定义二维碳化硅空位缺陷的量子性质中起关键作用。

Abstract: Bond reconstruction in vacancy-related structures affects their formation energies, symmetries, and electronic and optical properties. Using density functional theory, we investigate bond reconstruction mechanisms of monovacancies and vacancy aggregates in monolayer silicon carbide. Multiple bond descriptors reveal that isolated monovacancies undergo both in-plane reconstruction and out-of-plane distortion, which together shape their stability and electronic structure. For compact vacancy aggregates, we show that bond reconstruction acts as a key stabilization mechanism. However, in carbon monovacancy, reconstruction suppresses optical activity. In contrast, a highly stable aggregate composed of three carbon vacancies surrounding a silicon vacancy emerges as a promising infrared color-center candidate, combining a triplet ground state with a favorable Debye-Waller factor of the emission. These results highlight the role of bond reconstruction in defining the quantum properties of vacancy defects in two-dimensional silicon carbide.

</details>


### [3] [Instability-Avoiding Active Learning for Cluster Expansions in Complex Multielement Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16486)
*Michael J. Waters,James M. Rondinelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将稳定性分类集成到主动学习工作流中的方法，以自动避免为动态不稳定的结构生成训练数据，并在Ni-Fe-Cr-Al-Ti-Si FCC多主元合金体系中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团簇展开在复杂成分空间（如多主元合金）中研究化学无序时，训练成本随着化学物种数量迅速增长。许多成分空间中存在大范围晶格动力学不稳定的区域，这些区域的传统处理方式是先验回避，但生成不适用训练数据仍然昂贵。因此需要一种能自主避免不稳定结构计算的方法。

Method: 在主动学习工作流中集成稳定性分类程序，并利用基于马氏距离的结构选择策略来增强训练集的多样性，从而确保模型鲁棒性。以Ni-Fe-Cr-Al-Ti-Si FCC MPEA为基准，其中只有Ni和Al在FCC相中热力学稳定。

Result: 成功在复杂MPEA体系中实现了训练集的自适应扩展与不稳定结构的自动规避，验证了稳定性分类与主动学习结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出方法能够有效降低团簇展开在不稳定区域上的训练开销，提升模型在复杂合金成分空间中的训练效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: The high efficiency of cluster expansions make them appealing for studying chemical disorder in complex composition spaces such as in multi-principal element alloys (MPEAs). Several works have attempted to address the rapidly growing training cost with number of chemical species through active learning, transfer learning, and chemical embedding. However, many composition spaces have large regions where the host lattice becomes dynamical unstable, which are often avoided a priori so as to not generate expensive but inapplicable training data. Here, we demonstrate a procedure for integrating stability classification within an active learning workflow to autonomously avoid calculations for unstable structures. Our workflow augments the stability classification procedure with Mahalanobis distance-based structure selection to ensure model robustness by training set diversification. We benchmark our methods by training a cluster expansion for the complex FCC MPEA spanning the Ni-Fe-Cr-Al-Ti-Si alloy space, in which only Ni and Al are thermodynamically stable as FCC

</details>


### [4] [Harnessing disorder to decouple extension and shear in kirigami metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16583)
*Haomin Yu,Hanxun Jin,Mingxuan Bi,Mohammad Jafari,Feng Helen Long,Michael J Greenberg,Farid Alisafaei,Guy Genin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将工程化无序（随机kirigami）作为设计自由度，通过图神经网络（GNN）结合遗传算法逆向设计，实现可编程各向异性力学响应，并几乎完全消除拉伸-剪切耦合，从而突破周期性kirigami的力学性能限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性kirigami存在固有缺陷：拉伸-剪切耦合无法抑制，且各向异性刚度局限于狭窄、离散的响应范围，无法独立调控。受生物组织（如皮肤中梯度纤维取向、心肌中层析各向异性）通过受控无序实现方向依赖力学的启发，作者试图将无序工程化作为新的设计自由度来克服这些局限。

Method: 采用随机kirigami图案打破周期性约束；使用几何感知图神经网络（GNN）建立切割拓扑与完整非线性双向应力-应变响应之间的映射；结合遗传算法逆向设计出能沿两个垂直轴复现目标力学响应的图案。通过 fabricated 弹性体样品进行实验验证。

Result: 随机kirigami可访问比周期性图案更连续、更广阔的力学响应区域；实现了可编程各向异性并几乎完全消除了拉伸-剪切耦合；GNN训练速度比基于图像的模型快一个数量级且精度更高；实验制备的弹性体样品成功复现了预测的非线性各向异性响应。

Conclusion: 将无序转化为可控设计变量，开发出拉伸时无寄生剪切的超材料，可用于软体致动器、与各向异性生物组织匹配的组织接口器件等应用。

Abstract: Kirigami turns stiff sheets into compliant, shape-morphing structures, but its reliance on periodic cut patterns comes at a cost: correlated panel rotations couple extension to shear, so stretching one axis drives a parasitic shear that cannot be suppressed, and also confine anisotropic stiffness to a narrow, discrete set of responses that cannot be tuned independently. Biological tissues overcome an analogous constraint through controlled disorder, such as graded fiber orientations in skin and hierarchical anisotropy in myocardium, achieving direction-dependent mechanics unavailable to regular architectures. Here, we show that engineered disorder is a design degree of freedom for kirigami, with stochastic kirigami accessing a continuous and far broader region of mechanical response than periodic patterns. This includes programmable anisotropy with near-complete elimination of extension-shear coupling. Because disordered patterns lack a simple parameterization, we navigate this design space with a geometry-aware graph neural network (GNN) that maps cut topology to the full nonlinear, bidirectional stress-strain response, coupled to a genetic algorithm that inverse-designs patterns reproducing target responses along two perpendicular axes. The GNN trains an order of magnitude faster and more accurately than image-based models. Fabricated elastomer samples reproduce the predicted nonlinear, anisotropic responses, closing the loop from design to physical component. By turning disorder into a variable to control directional stiffness, this work develops architected materials that stretch without parasitic shear, from soft actuators to tissue-interfacing devices matched to the anisotropy of living tissue.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Janus Atomic Ordering: High-Throughput First-Principles Search for Hidden MoSO Monolayer Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16589)
*Zhijing Huang,Tingting Zeng,Lin Zhang,Zhibin Gao,Longyuzhi Xu,Li Yang,Shuming Zeng,Zonglin Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过高通量第一性原理计算，从1325种候选结构中筛选出三种新型非Janus结构的2D MoSO单层（Reversed 2H-MoSO、Hybrid 2H-MoSO和Hybrid 1T'-MoSO），发现Hybrid 1T'-MoSO具有金属特性，且应力/曲率可调控其半导体-半金属-金属相变，其中Hybrid 1T'-MoSO的HER催化活性优异（ΔG = -0.002 eV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D MoSO研究仅局限于传统Janus结构，缺乏对其他结构类型的探索，亟需拓展该材料家族并发现具有新颖物理化学性质的同素异形体。

Method: 采用高通量第一性原理计算，结合随机采样策略、图论和群论，从1325种候选结构中系统筛选稳定的非Janus MoSO单层，并通过动力学、热力学和力学稳定性分析及AIMD模拟进行验证。

Result: 筛选出三种稳定非Janus MoSO单层；其结合能低于Janus结构（-4.38至-4.51 eV/atom）；AIMD表明热稳定性超过2000 K；Hybrid 1T'-MoSO呈金属性；应变和曲率可诱导半导体-半金属-金属相变；Hybrid 1T'-MoSO的HER吉布斯自由能为-0.002 eV。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了2D MoSO材料家族，并证明高通量计算结合图论/群论策略是发现新型稳定二维功能材料的可靠方法，Hybrid 1T'-MoSO在析氢催化方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Despite the growing interest in two-dimensional (2D) MoSO systems, existing studies have exclusively focused on conventional Janus structures. In this work, we perform high-throughput first-principles calculations to explore novel stable 2D MoSO monolayers. Combined with random sampling strategy, graph theory and group theory, we successfully screen out three novel non-Janus 2D MoSO monolayers from 1325 candidate structures, namely Reversed 2H-MoSO, Hybrid 2H-MoSO, and Hybrid 1T'-MoSO. Compared with Janus MoSO monolayers, the non-Janus MoSO counterparts possess lower binding energies, varying from -4.38 to -4.51 eV/atom. A systematic combination of dynamic, thermodynamic, and mechanical stability analyses corroborates their excellent structural robustness. Ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations confirm their superior thermal resistance, with the structures remaining stable at temperatures beyond 2000 K. Interestingly, unlike the semiconducting Janus MoSO, the Hybrid 1T'-MoSO monolayer exhibits distinct metallic characteristics. Furthermore, we found that strain and curvature can enable controlled phase transitions of MoSO among semiconducting, semimetallic, and metallic phases. More importantly, the Hybrid 1T'-MoSO exhibits favorable HER activity with a Gibbs free energy of -0.002 eV, rendering it a promising candidate for hydrogen evolution catalysis. This work not only expands the family of 2D MoSO materials but also provides a reliable strategy for discovering stable functional 2D materials via high-throughput computation.

</details>


### [6] [High-entropy perovskites, architectured by s0/d0/d10 cations, as novel electrolytes for solid oxide fuel cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16616)
*Ho Truong Nam Hai,Rishad Kunafiev,Kwati Leonard,Kaveh Edalati*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过高压扭转加工和煅烧合成了三种高熵氧化物钙钛矿电解质材料，其中混合s0/d0/d10阳离子的(Ba0.50Sr0.50)(Ti0.33Zr0.33Sn0.33)O₃在质子型固体氧化物燃料电池中表现出最佳性能，最大功率密度达0.53 W·cm⁻²（973 K），归因于其异质电子结构和优化的局域金属-氧键长及外在氧空位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体氧化物燃料电池阴极、阳极和电解质材料可用性有限，尤其是稀土金属依赖性问题亟待解决。

Method: 采用高压扭转加工（HPT）和煅烧顺序烧结合成三种高熵钙钛矿：s0/d0型(Ba₀.₅₀Sr₀.₅₀)(Ti₀.₃₃Zr₀.₃₃Hf₀.₃₃)O₃、s0/d10型(Ba₀.₅₀Sr₀.₅₀)(Ga₀.₃₃In₀.₃₃Sn₀.₃₃)O₃和混合s0/d0/d10型(Ba₀.₅₀Sr₀.₅₀)(Ti₀.₃₃Zr₀.₃₃Sn₀.₃₃)O₃。将其印刷（6-10 μm）在Ni-SrZr₀.₅Ce₀.₄Y₀.₁O₃阳极基底上，并涂覆Ba₀.₅La₀.₅CoO₃阴极。

Result: (Ba₀.₅₀Sr₀.₅₀)(Ti₀.₃₃Zr₀.₃₃Sn₀.₃₃)O₃性能最优：漏电流可忽略、欧姆电阻最低，最大功率密度0.53 W·cm⁻²（973 K）。同步辐射X射线吸收和光电子能谱分析表明其优异性能与异质电子结构（定制的未占据d轨道态）、有利的局域金属-氧键长和外在氧空位相关。

Conclusion: 混合s0/d0/d10阳离子的高熵钙钛矿可作为新型无稀土金属离子传导电解质，对质子型固体氧化物燃料电池具有重要意义。

Abstract: Solid oxide fuel cells enable efficient conversion of hydrogen into electricity. However, the limited availability of materials for their cathode, anode, and electrolyte remains a concern. This study introduces three high-entropy oxide perovskites as novel electrolyte materials for fuel cells (Ba0.50Sr0.50)(Ti0.33Zr0.33Hf0.33)O3 with s0/d0 cations, (Ba0.50Sr0.50)(Ga0.33In0.33Sn0.33)O3 with s0/d10 cations, and (Ba0.50Sr0.50)(Ti0.33Zr0.33Sn0.33)O3 with mixed s0/d0/d10 cations. Through sequential sintering by high-pressure torsion processing and calcination, these perovskites were synthesized and then printed with a thickness of about 6-10 microns on a Ni-SrZr0.5Ce0.4Y0.1O3 substrate as an anode and then coated with Ba0.5La0.5CoO3 as a cathode. Electrochemical analysis and impedance spectroscopy show that (Ba0.50Sr0.50)(Ti0.33Zr0.33Sn0.33)O3 with s0/d0/d10 cations exhibits the best performance with negligible current leakage and lowest ohmic resistance, while its maximum power density reaches 0.53 W.cm-2 at 973 K. Complementary synchrotron X-ray absorption and photoelectron spectroscopy analyses indicate that the superior performance of (Ba0.50Sr0.50)(Ti0.33Zr0.33Sn0.33)O3 correlates with its heterogeneous electronic structure characterized by tailored unoccupied d-orbital states, and favorable local metal-oxygen bond lengths and extrinsic oxygen vacancies. This investigation demonstrates the significance of high-entropy perovskites with mixed s0/d0/d10 cations as new rare-earth metal-free ion-conducting electrolytes, particularly for protonic solid oxide fuel cells.

</details>


### [7] [Three Million Years Opposite State Data Retention in Partially Switched Wurtzite Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16665)
*Maike Gremmel,Roberto Guido,Victor Witte,Uwe Schröder,Simon Fichtner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于纤锌矿结构铁电体的铁电存储器在150°C下可保持信息超过300万年。通过部分切换极化而非完全切换，可显著降低初始印迹变化，从而将反向态保持力提高5-7个数量级，同时提升器件的耐久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索纤锌矿结构铁电体（如AlScN和AlScBN）在铁电存储器中的应用潜力，解决高自发极化材料中存在的初始印迹变化问题，并提升器件在高温下的保持力和耐久性。

Method: 将标准畴壁运动受限的开关动力学与近电极注入模型相结合，研究不同厚度（60-270 nm）和不同化合物（AlScN和AlScBN）薄膜的部分切换行为，分析其对初始印迹、切换极化和保持力的影响。

Result: 部分切换可有效限制初始印迹变化；与完全切换相比，部分切换后相同时间内的切换极化更大；反向态保持力提升5-7个数量级；同时能延缓极化损失和击穿，增强器件耐久性。

Conclusion: 部分切换策略是改善高自发极化铁电器件（尤其是纤锌矿结构化合物）运行性能的有效方法，可在保持力、耐久性和可靠性之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Ferroelectric memories based on the wurtzite-structured ferroelectrics are projected to store information for more than 3 million years at 150C. These results are extracted by combining standard domain wall motion limited switching kinetics with the near-by-electrode injection model for opposite state retention in ferroelectric random access memory. This impressive performance is greatly aided by switching only a fraction of the total polarization to store data, in order to limit the initial imprint variation of the devices - an effect that is universally observed in films with different thicknesses (60 nm - 270 nm) and different compounds (AlScN and AlScBN). Paradoxically, yet systematically, this reduction in initial imprint consistently results in larger switching polarization after a given time, compared to the fully switching state and 5-7 orders of magnitude improved opposite state retention. Finally, partial switching is able to simultaneously boost endurance against premature polarization loss and breakdown, making it a promising strategy for improved operation of ferroelectric devices with large spontaneous polarization, in particularly wurtzite-structured compounds.

</details>


### [8] [Lattice initialisation and finite-size effects of non-equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations for heat transfer across graphene-copper interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16783)
*L. A. van Goor,W. N. Edeling,D. Jafari,H. Lee,E. Luesink,W. W. Wits,A. V. Lyulin,B. J. Geurts*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用非平衡分子动力学模拟研究了铜-石墨烯-铜界面的热传输，发现晶格初始化策略导致的残余应变差异可使Kapitza热阻产生两倍变化，且声子谱重叠增加反而对应更高的热阻，表明界面热传输不能仅由谱重叠解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中NEMD模拟已识别出石墨烯-铜界面Kapitza热阻的趋势，但模拟结果对域构型（如晶格初始化、石墨烯褶皱）的依赖性尚未充分探索，这些构型选择可能严重影响结果的可靠性和一致性。

Method: 采用非平衡分子动力学（NEMD）模拟，系统改变域长度、域构型（两种常规晶格初始化策略）及边界温度条件，分析热导率和Kapitza热阻的变化，并结合声子谱分析和局部结构无序度表征。

Result: 1) 两种晶格初始化策略导致Kapitza热阻相差两倍，尽管晶格参数差异仅百分之几；2) 低残余应变下Kapitza热阻反而更高，与声子谱重叠增加的预期相反；3) 界面附近出现阻尼边界层，表现为局部结构无序和谱展宽；4) 铜晶格热导率表现出明显的域尺寸和温度依赖性，符合声子平均自由程限制。

Conclusion: 晶格初始化引起的残余应变是影响Kapitza热阻的关键因素，声子谱重叠不足以单独描述界面热传输动力学；界面阻尼边界层的形成是导致热阻增加的重要机制；域长度和边界温度对Kapitza热阻无显著影响，但显著影响铜晶格热导率。

Abstract: We study thermal transport across copper-graphene-copper interfaces using Non-Equilibrium Molecular Dynamics (NEMD), focusing on the influence of finite domain length and domain configuration, including lattice initialisation and associated graphene wrinkling, on the predicted thermal conductivity and Kapitza resistance. In the literature, NEMD simulations identified trends in the Kapitza resistance of graphene-copper interfaces. However, the simulation outcomes and reliability may depend heavily on configuration choices that are underexplored in the literature. For elemental Ti, the calculated electrical resistivity agrees with independent \textsc{abinit} electron--phonon calculations and experiment, and also reproduces the high-temperature saturation near the Mott--Ioffe--Regel limit. Under electron--phonon nonequilibrium, the electronic thermal conductivity saturates and then decreases with electronic temperature, reaching a maximum of about \SI{2.97}{\kilo\watt\per\metre\per\kelvin} in Ti but only \SI{0.47}{\kilo\watt\per\metre\per\kelvin} in Ti-6Al-4V, a factor of 6.4 lower. In two-temperature-model simulations the alloy and elemental parameter sets yield peak lattice temperatures differing by only about 1.4\%, consistent with reported experimental ablation thresholds that differ by about 3\%, well within their measurement uncertainties. Replacing the first-principles thermal conductivity with the low-temperature Drude limit shifts the peak lattice temperature by up to 19\%, showing that the functional form of the transport model is even more important than the elemental vs alloy distinction for predictive accuracy.

Method: 1) 构建MC3D数据库并与OQMD、MP及实验形成焓对比；2) 使用r²SCAN级别的基础MLIP（PET-OMATPES）改进形成能，无需额外DFT计算；3) 在delta学习框架中训练经典机器学习模型，利用MLIP的潜变量特征进一步修正形成能。

Conclusion: 验证了将PBEsol几何结构与meta-GGA能量结合的传统方法推广到基础MLIP时代，证明基础MLIP和delta学习可有效提升DFT形成能的实验一致性。

Abstract: Crystal structure databases curated by high-throughput density functional theory calculations typically serve as the starting point for computational materials discovery efforts. Thermodynamic stability data, such as formation energies and the energy above the convex hull, are important quantities to guide the search for novel materials, enabling filtering for (meta)stable structures. Here, we present the thermodynamic stability of the fully open-source, reproducible, and experimentally focused Materials Cloud three-dimensional crystals database (MC3D). We compare against two other DFT databases, the Open Quantum Materials Database (OQMD) and the Materials Project (MP), as well as against experimental formation enthalpies. We then demonstrate how recent foundational machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) trained at the r$^2$SCAN level (specifically, we test PET-OMATPES here) can be leveraged to improve the agreement of formation energies with experiment, reducing the mean absolute error by more than 40% relative to GGA without requiring any additional DFT calculation. Our results validate and extend the established practice of combining PBEsol geometries with meta-GGA energies to the era of foundational MLIPs. Finally, we train classical machine learning models to further correct the formation energies in a delta-learning framework, where we use the information-rich latent features of the foundational MLIP. These models further reduce the mean absolute error below 50 meV/atom, bringing it down to values comparable with the experimental uncertainty itself. Notably, compared to purely compositional features, the latent features (combined with carefully tuned regularization) simultaneously reduce the prediction error and limit the impact of the learned corrections on the relative phase stability.

Abstract: Polaron formation localizes charge carriers and drives a crossover from band-like to hopping transport in materials. The approach optimizes transition states between self-trapped polaron states directly in reciprocal space and provides the polaron configurations along the path, enabling evaluation of adiabatic hopping rates and mobilities. We apply the method to LiF and rutile TiO$_2$, revealing multi-step and anisotropic hopping mechanisms. In rutile TiO$_2$, the computed electron-polaron mobility agrees with experiment, whereas band-like Boltzmann transport substantially overestimates the mobility. Our results establish a scalable route to first-principles polaron-hopping dynamics in materials in which charge motion is governed by self-trapping.

Abstract: Refractory high-entropy alloys have emerged as promising candidates for high-temperature applications due to their exceptional mechanical properties. Understanding the thermodynamic mechanisms underlying chemical ordering in these complex systems is critical for optimizing their performance. In this work, by utilizing a universal machine learning interatomic potential with hybrid Monte Carlo and molecular dynamics simulations, the temperature-dependent thermodynamics and mechanical properties of (MoCrTi)(100-x)Alx system have been investigated. The heat capacities and short-range order parameters reveal distinct order-disorder transition behaviors. While the Mo25Cr25Ti25Al25 and Mo32Cr32Ti32Al4 alloys exhibit a single transition dominated by the synergistic ordering of B2-type atomic pairs, the Mo28Cr28Ti28Al16 and Mo30Cr30Ti30Al10 alloys display two separate transitions: a low-temperature stage driven by specific pairs (Mo-Al in Mo28Cr28Ti28Al16; Al-Al in Mo30Cr30Ti30Al10) and a high-temperature stage governed by the remaining pairs. Structural analysis indicates that in the low-temperature ordered B2 phase, Mo and Al share one sublattice while Cr and Ti share the other. Furthermore, the relationship between ordering and mechanical stiffness has been identified. Ordering significantly enhances the elastic constants and moduli, and gives rise to a non-monotonic compositional dependence. Unlike random solid solutions, where stiffness increases monotonically with decreasing Al content, ordered configurations exhibit a non-monotonic trend, peaking at the Mo30Cr30Ti30Al10 alloy. This enhancement is attributed to an optimized population of stiff atomic pairs induced by strong short-range order. These findings provide fundamental insights into the interplay between compositions, chemical ordering, and mechanical performance, offering guidance for the design of refractory high-entropy alloys.

Abstract: The mechanical reliability of nitrogen-doped graphene is often attributed to its nitrogen content, yet nitrogen occurs in chemically distinct configurations whose individual mechanical roles, and whose interactions with other defects, remain unresolved. Here, molecular dynamics simulations of uniaxial tension are used to separate the contributions of nitrogen chemistry, missing atoms, and defect arrangement to the strength and fracture of graphene. Three size-matched defects, a graphitic-nitrogen cluster, a void, and a pyridinic-nitrogen cluster, are compared so that two controlled contrasts isolate the effects of edge chemistry and of the vacancy independently. The graphitic cluster leaves the mechanical properties essentially unchanged (a strength reduction of <1 %), whereas the void degrades the ultimate strength by ~23 % and the pyridinic cluster, which combines the same vacancy with edge nitrogen, is the most damaging (~30 %), failing abruptly from its nitrogen-decorated rim rather than through the damage-tolerant process of the bare void. The mechanical impact of a nitrogen cluster is therefore governed by whether it carries vacancies, not by nitrogen itself. When a nitrogen cluster and a void coexist, their interaction is controlled by orientation relative to the load: in-line defects interact negligibly and fail at the more severe member, whereas side-by-side defects couple through overlapping stress fields and weaken the sheet progressively as they approach, an interaction that persists to separations of ~80 Å. These results establish that the mechanical integrity of nitrogen-modified graphene is determined by the configuration of defects, the bonding environment of nitrogen, and the arrangement of coexisting defects relative to the load, rather than by nitrogen content or defect density alone, thereby providing a basis for defect-tolerant design.

Comprehensive screening of 22 doping elements identified Ni as the most effective dopant. Motivation: 共价虚拟筛选需要同时评估配体的非共价识别能力和形成反应竞争构象的能力，现有方法在这两方面的结合存在不足，且计算成本较高。

Method: BCover将预反应对接与量子化学导出的配体反应性描述符以及蛋白口袋静电性质相结合，使用非线性树基评分模型聚合量子化学描述符，实现共价配体优先排序。

Result: 在COValid基准上，BCover平均调整LogAUC为31%（最大57%），平均ROC-AUC为0.88（最大0.96），平均EF1为18（最大33）。平均LogAUC优于AutoDock、DOCK6、DOCKovalent及AlphaFold3-Rosetta，但AlphaFold3-mPAE富集效果最强。BCover每配体约10秒，比AF3工作流快约25倍，时间调整后的虚拟筛选生产力指数最高。重对接实验能恢复近天然配体构象。

Conclusion: 结合对接几何、配体局部电子反应性和口袋静电性质是一种高效且可解释的共价配体优先排序策略。BCover可作为高通量筛选方法，补充后续先导优化中计算成本更高的QM/MM和自由能计算。

Abstract: Covalent virtual screening requires ranking compounds according to both noncovalent recognition and their ability to adopt a reaction-competent geometry with an appropriately reactive warhead. Here, we introduce BCover, a reaction-aware scoring suite that combines pre-reactive docking with quantum-chemistry-derived ligand reactivity descriptors, the electrostatic properties of the protein pocket. Quantum-chemical descriptors are aggregated using a nonlinear tree-based scoring model. BCover was evaluated retrospectively on the COValid benchmark, comprising nine targets and ten reactive sites, and compared with AutoDock, DOCK6, DOCKovalent, AlphaFold3 with Rosetta rescoring, and AlphaFold3 confidence-based ranking. BCover achieved an average adjusted LogAUC of 31% (57% max), an average ROC-AUC of 0.88 (0.96 max), an average EF1 of 18 (33 max). Its average LogAUC exceeded those of the classical docking methods and AlphaFold3-Rosetta, although AlphaFold3-mPAE provided the strongest overall enrichment. At an average runtime of about 10~s per ligand, BCover was approximately 25-fold faster than the evaluated AlphaFold3 workflows and achieved the highest average time-adjusted virtual-screening productivity index. Redocking experiments further showed that the method recovered near-native ligand conformations. These results demonstrate that combining docking-derived geometry with ligand local electronic reactivity and pocket electrostatics provides an efficient and interpretable strategy for covalent ligand prioritization. BCover is intended as a high-throughput screening method that complements more computationally demanding QM/MM and free-energy calculations during subsequent lead optimization.

</details>

### [25] [Some Large Language Models Exhibit Consistent Risk Attitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16197)
*Bowen Sun,Rui Min,Yuxi Wang,Brian Odegaard,Qi Wang,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个跨领域框架，将情境风险信念与类别决策解耦，测试了六个代表性LLM和100名人类参与者在空间导航、临床分诊和金融分配任务中的风险态度。研究发现LLM表现出：(i) 任务内一致性，(ii) 跨领域排序稳定性，(iii) 相对于人类基线收敛于更受限的风险态度分布。这表明风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且此前未被表征的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放、高风险的AI部署场景中，感知风险如何转化为行动这一关键维度尚未被衡量。现有研究缺乏对LLM在不确定性下风险态度的系统性和一致性评估，因此需要建立量化框架来表征和评估AI系统的风险决策行为。

Method: 提出一个跨领域框架，将情境风险信念（contextual risk belief）与类别决策（categorical decision）解耦。对六个代表性LLM和100名人类参与者施加空间导航、临床分诊和金融分配三种任务。使用回归模型提取每个智能体从信念到决策的映射，量化风险敏感度和风险态度偏差。

Result: 大多数测试的LLM表现出：(i) 稳健的任务内一致性——在固定任务领域内信念到风险决策的映射稳定；(ii) 跨领域排序稳定性——风险姿态在不同任务间保持相对排序；(iii) 相对于人类基线，LLM的风险态度分布收敛于更窄的范围。揭示了风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且此前未被表征的维度。

Conclusion: 风险态度是LLM行为中一个稳定且此前未被表征的维度，这为评估和对齐开放决策场景下的AI系统建立了基础，并激励进一步研究这些内在行为倾向的起源。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems are deployed in open-ended, high-stakes settings, a critical dimension remains unmeasured: how perceived risk is translated into action. We test whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic and consistent risk attitudes under uncertainty. We introduce a cross-domain framework that decouples contextual risk belief from categorical decision, and apply it to six representative LLMs and 100 human participants across spatial navigation, clinical triage, and financial allocation tasks. Using regression models, we extract each agents belief-to-decision mapping and quantify risk sensitivity and risk attitude bias. We find that most tested LLMs exhibit (i) robust intra-task consistency, indicating stable mappings from contextual belief to risk decision within a fixed task domain; (ii) cross-domain rank-order stability, preserving relative risk posture across tasks; and (iii) a convergence toward a restricted risk-attitude distribution relative to the broader human baseline. These results reveal risk attitude as a stable and previously uncharacterized dimension of LLM behavior, establishing a foundation for evaluating and aligning AI systems in open-ended decision-making and motivating further investigation into the origins of these intrinsic behavioral dispositions.

</details>


### [26] [PlanFlip: Attacking Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Planning-Phase Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16199)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanFlip框架揭示了多智能体LLM系统中规划阶段的提示注入攻击（四种攻击类型），发现能力更强的模型（如GPT-5）反而更易受攻击（ASR=0.68），同质化流水线存在相关智能体盲点，而推理增强型模型（DeepSeek-R1）具有天然抵抗力。提出两种检测方法（D1和D2），核心洞见：异构模型多样性是安全前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的规划阶段是关键攻击面，单次注入即可实现级联放大，污染所有下游子任务。现有研究忽视了这一阶段的攻击向量。

Method: 提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击：GoalSubstitution (PF-1)、PriorityInversion (PF-2)、ContextPollution (PF-3)和RoleConfusion (PF-4)，每种攻击伪装成合理的工具输出以规避关键词过滤。在9个前沿LLM上进行3,479个回合的评估。

Result: (1) GPT-5攻击成功率最高(ASR=0.68)，能力越强越脆弱；(2) 同质化流水线存在相关智能体盲点，GPT-4o和Llama-3.3-70B的ASR接近0但Stealth=1.00且StepShift>0，攻击重构了计划而同一骨干网的Critic报告对齐；(3) DeepSeek-R1在所有攻击下StepShift=0.00。提出的D1和D2检测方法在16个单元中15个优于同骨干基线。

Conclusion: 异构模型多样性是多智能体系统的安全前提，同质化骨干的冗余无法抵御规划阶段攻击。推理增强型模型具有天然抗注入能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on a Planner to decompose goals into sub-task sequences that downstream Executor and Critic agents execute and audit. We identify the planning phase as a critical attack surface: a single injection into the Planner's context achieves cascade amplification, corrupting all downstream sub-tasks simultaneously. We introduce PlanFlip, a framework comprising four planning-phase prompt injection attacks -- GoalSubstitution (PF-1), PriorityInversion (PF-2), ContextPollution (PF-3), and RoleConfusion (PF-4) -- each disguised as plausible tool outputs to evade keyword filters. Evaluating nine frontier LLMs across 3,479 episodes, we uncover three findings: (1) capability amplifies vulnerability -- GPT-5 achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR = 0.68), contradicting the assumption that stronger models are inherently more secure; (2) homogeneous pipelines exhibit a correlated-agent blind spot -- GPT-4o and Llama-3.3-70B show ASR near 0 yet Stealth = 1.00 and StepShift > 0, with attacks restructuring plans while the same-backbone Critic reports alignment (two independent judges confirm -0.20 to -0.32 semantic deviation, r = 0.943); (3) reasoning-augmented models resist injections -- DeepSeek-R1 achieves StepShift = 0.00 across all attacks. We propose GoalAnchorCheck (D1) and CrossAgentConsensus (D2), achieving detection rates up to 1.00 and outperforming same-backbone baselines in 15 of 16 cells. Our key insight: heterogeneous model diversity is a security prerequisite for multi-agent systems; redundancy within a homogeneous backbone provides no protection against planning-phase attacks.

</details>


### [27] [Deterministic Replay for AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16200)
*Rasheed Mudasiru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: agrepl 是一个开发者优先的 CLI 框架，通过 MITM 代理拦截外部交互、序列化为结构化执行轨迹，并在完全隔离的环境中重放，实现了 AI 代理系统的确定性重放，重放保真度 F=1.0，每步延迟中位数降低 98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 代理系统因 LLM 采样方差、外部 API 状态、CDN 基础设施头信息和执行环境噪声等因素具有内在非确定性，导致任何先前的代理运行都无法被忠实地重新执行；现有的可观测性平台虽能捕获执行日志，但无法在隔离环境中复现一次运行。

Method: 在传输层通过中间人(MITM)代理拦截所有外部交互，将其序列化为结构化执行轨迹，在严格隔离（零出站网络访问）的环境中重放；形式化定义了代理执行模型，提出了请求键匹配函数 K(s)，证明了确定性不变量；引入噪声感知的 diff 算法，将 HTTP 头部分歧分类为信号层和噪声层。

Result: 在五个工作负载上的实证评估（n=250 个重放实例）显示重放保真度 F=1.0，每步延迟中位数降低 98.3%。

Conclusion: agrepl 以 Go 语言实现，以单一静态二进制文件形式发布，采用 MIT 许可证开源，为 AI 代理系统提供了一种有效的确定性重放解决方案。

Abstract: AI agent systems that couple large language models (LLMs) with external tools and APIs are inherently non-deterministic: LLM sampling variance, external API state, CDN infrastructure headers, and execution-environment noise collectively prevent any prior agent run from being faithfully re-executed. Existing observability platforms capture execution logs but cannot reproduce a run in isolation. We present agrepl, a developer-first CLI framework for deterministic replay of agent executions. agrepl intercepts all external interactions at the transport layer via a man-in-the-middle (MITM) proxy, serialises them as structured execution traces, and replays them in a strictly isolated environment with zero outbound network access. We formalise the agent execution model, define the request-key matching function K(s), and prove the determinism invariant. We introduce a noise-aware diff algorithm classifying HTTP header divergence into signal and noise tiers. Empirical evaluation across five workloads (n = 250 replay instances) demonstrates replay fidelity F = 1.0 and a median per-step latency reduction of 98.3%. agrepl is implemented in Go, ships as a single static binary, and is released under the MIT licence.
  Keywords: AI agents, deterministic replay, LLM debugging, reproducibility, MITM proxy, execution tracing, record/replay systems.

</details>


### [28] [PPO-HSC: An Exploratory Reinforcement Learning Framework Based on Wide-Area Policy Coverage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16206)
*Yujie Shen,Haowen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PPO-HSC通过在标准RLVR框架中引入高阶采样覆盖（HSC）奖励机制，激励LLM在微调过程中探索"低相似性但高有效性"的推理模式，有效缓解了模式 collapse 问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上提升了解决方案多样性与状态空间覆盖率，同时保持或超越了现有最优RL基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR在LLM微调中虽然能有效强化高奖励轨迹，但容易导致模型过度优化已知解，牺牲探索能力，陷入模式坍塌（"无形枷锁"），限制了模型对更广泛解空间的探索能力。

Method: 提出PPO-HSC框架，核心组件包括：(1) 高阶采样覆盖（HSC）奖励，激励发现低相似性但高有效性的推理模式；(2) 动态轨迹库，存储已验证的独特解；(3) 可微语义新颖性奖励信号；(4) 合理性约束，确保结构理性。整体基于PPO算法进行优化。

Result: 在数学推理（GSM8K、SVAMP）和代码生成任务上的实验表明，PPO-HSC显著提升了解决方案多样性和状态空间覆盖率，同时保持或超越了当前最优RL基线的准确率和语法完整性。

Conclusion: PPO-HSC有效解决了LLM微调中的模式坍塌问题，通过鼓励探索多样性解空间，实现了探索与利用的更好平衡，为提升LLM的泛化能力和创造性推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces PPO-HSC (Proximal Policy Optimization with High-order Sampling Coverage), an exploratory reinforcement learning framework designed to address the "Invisible Shackles" of mode collapse in Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning. While standard Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) effectively reinforces high-reward trajectories, it often leads models to over-optimize known solutions, sacrificing curiosity and the ability to explore broader solution manifolds. To overcome this, PPO-HSC incorporates a High-order Sampling Coverage (HSC) reward that incentivizes the discovery of "low-similarity yet high-validity" reasoning patterns. By maintaining a dynamic trajectory library of verified unique solutions, the framework provides a differentiable signal that rewards semantic novelty while ensuring structural rationality through a plausibility constraint. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K, SVAMP) and code generation tasks demonstrate that PPO-HSC significantly enhances solution diversity and state-space coverage while maintaining or surpassing the accuracy and syntax integrity of state-of-the-art RL baselines.

</details>


### [29] [Accurate and Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16211)
*Zicheng Zhao,Xinyang Guo,Luyao Lv,Menghan Wang,Ming Li,Shuaicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个结构化、冲突感知的长期记忆框架，通过实体类型图存储、哈希加速双路径检索和主动冲突检测，在长对话QA任务中达到89.35%准确率（+27.21 pp），冲突检测率达66%（4.7倍于基线），且检索延迟低至0.58秒/问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的持久记忆系统存在两个关键缺陷：扁平化非结构化存储丢失了多跳和时间推理所需的上下文关系，以及依赖昂贵的LLM分类导致延迟敏感部署不可行，且缺乏验证机制导致矛盾累积。

Method: 提出三种核心能力：(1)实体类型图存储与语义分类，保留事件、角色和关系的关系结构；(2)哈希加速双路径检索，用局部敏感哈希替代LLM分类，实现近瞬时查找；(3)保存时主动冲突检测，交叉引用新信息与图中邻居，触发矛盾条目的更新或删除。

Result: 在LoCoMo上准确率89.35%（+27.21 pp）；HaluMem-Medium/Long提取F1分别为86.77%和85.84%，QA正确率73.10%和70.75%；检测66%注入事实冲突（基线14%）；平均搜索延迟0.58秒/问题。

Conclusion: MOSAIC通过结构化图存储与高效检索机制，同时提升了长期记忆的准确性和效率，在多项基准上显著超越现有方法，有效解决了矛盾累积问题。

Abstract: LLM agents augmented with persistent memory can recall past interactions, but existing systems suffer from two limitations: flat, unstructured storage loses relational context needed for multi-hop and temporal reasoning, and reliance on expensive LLM-based classification makes them impractical for latency-sensitive deployment. Without mechanisms to validate new information against stored knowledge, these systems silently accumulate contradictions. We present MOSAIC (Memory-Organized Structured Agent for Information Collection), a structured, conflict-aware long-term memory framework for LLM agents that is substantially more accurate and efficient. MOSAIC introduces three key capabilities: (1) entity-typed graph storage with semantic classification preserving relational structure across events, personas, and relationships, enabling multi-hop and temporal reasoning over conversation history; (2) hash-accelerated dual-path retrieval replacing LLM-based classification with locality-sensitive hashing, achieving near-instantaneous lookup with negligible accuracy loss; and (3) active conflict detection at save time that cross-references new information against existing graph neighbors, triggering updates or deletions for contradictory entries. Evaluated on LoCoMo (long-conversation QA), HaluMem, and a novel clinical-guideline error compounding test, MOSAIC achieves 89.35% accuracy on LoCoMo (+27.21 pp over the best baseline), best HaluMem-Medium extraction F1(86.77%) and HaluMem-Long extraction F1 (85.84%), best QA correctness on both Medium and Long (73.10%, 70.75%), and detects 66% of injected factual conflicts-4.7 times higher than the best baseline (14%)-while hash-accelerated retrieval keeps average search latency at 0.58 s per question.

</details>


### [30] [Symbolic Augmentation Closes a Canonical-Equivalence Blind Spot in Neural Fact-Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16212)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出符号增强框架，通过逆向运行符号验证器生成标签保持的训练数据，解决大语言模型在科学摘要中数量单位幻觉问题，将规范等价鲁棒性从36.5%提升至98.2%，微调ModernBERT达到宏F1=0.902。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在总结科学文本时经常对数字和单位产生幻觉，这种错误可能无声地颠倒科学主张。现有神经事实核查器无法有效检测此类错误，特别是在物理等价量（如95°C和368.15K）上表现脆弱。

Method: 提出了五类带类型数量错误分类体系（typed-quantity error taxonomy）和1500项基准；采用ModernBERT编码器微调；核心创新是符号增强（Symbolic Augmentation）——在训练时逆向运行符号验证器模块生成标签保持的增强数据。

Result: 微调ModernBERT在基准上宏F1=0.899，但对规范等价重写准确率暴跌至36.5%；符号增强将规范等价鲁棒性提升至98.2%，宏F1提升至0.902；外部SciFact-Open基准上宏F1从0.791提升至0.828。符号特征作为辅助编码器输入无增益，符号银标签在教师噪声下负面缩放。

Conclusion: 训练时增强是符号组件与学习组件之间正确的集成点，该方法以零推理成本匹配封闭前沿大语言模型性能。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate numbers and units when summarizing scientific text, a failure mode that can silently invert a scientific claim. We recast the detection of such errors as typed verification: we introduce a five-class typed-quantity error taxonomy and a 1500-item benchmark, rewritten from PMC and arXiv sources and labeled by two independent LLM annotators with adjudication (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.882). A ModernBERT encoder fine-tuned on this benchmark reaches macro-F1 = 0.899, far above any off-the-shelf neural fact-checker, yet four probes expose a sharp structural blind spot: on canonical-equivalent rewrites of physically equivalent quantities (e.g., 95°C and 368.15 K) its accuracy collapses to 36.5%. We propose Symbolic Augmentation, a training-time framework that runs the modules of a symbolic verifier in reverse to generate label-preserving augmented training data. The augmentation lifts canonical-equivalence robustness to 98.2% while slightly improving in-distribution accuracy (macro-F1: 0.899 to 0.902); the augmented encoder matches a closed-frontier LLM at no inference cost and transfers to an external benchmark (SciFact-Open binary macro-F1: 0.791 to 0.828). Two negative results sharpen the claim: symbolic features as auxiliary encoder inputs add nothing, and symbolic silver labels scale negatively under teacher noise. Together these results identify training-time augmentation as the right integration point between symbolic and learned components.

</details>


### [31] [RAIL Guard: Closing the Evaluation-to-Remediation Gap in Responsible AI for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16215)
*Sumit Verma,Pritam Prasun,Pritish Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAIL Guard 是一个闭环负责任的AI管道，通过评估-重写-再评估循环对LLM输出进行多维度评估和迭代修复，在8个可测量维度上实现了96.9%的收敛率（对比传统拦截重试的49.1%），同时识别出可修复维度与结构性维度的关键区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防护系统仅作为二元分类器拦截不安全内容，组织只能丢弃失败输出并从头重试，缺乏有效的闭环修复机制，导致效率低下。

Method: 构建了RAIL Guard闭环管道，包含评估-重写-再评估迭代循环，在8个维度上对LLM输出进行测量；通过四个前沿LLM、4,276个内容输出和6,400个工具调用场景进行三项实验评估。

Result: 闭环修复收敛率96.9%（vs 拦截重试49.1%）；反馈驱动自修复在可修复维度上达86.6%收敛且无显著效用损失（p=0.177）；工具调用前评估减少33%不安全执行（p=0.007）且不影响任务完成；识别出可修复维度与结构性维度（透明性93.0%、问责制92.8%、包容性82.5%失败率）的区分。

Conclusion: 闭环修复显著优于传统拦截重试方法；反馈驱动自修复在保持效用的同时有效修复问题；某些维度需要架构级而非算法级解决方案；该方案作为开源SDK发布。

Abstract: Existing guardrail systems for large language model agents operate as binary classifiers that block unsafe content, leaving organizations to discard failing outputs and retry from scratch. We introduce RAIL Guard, a closed-loop responsible AI pipeline that evaluates LLM outputs across eight measurable dimensions and iteratively remediates failing outputs through an evaluate-rewrite-reevaluate loop. We evaluate the pipeline across three experiments on four frontier LLMs and 4,276 content outputs plus 6,400 agent tool-call scenarios. Closed-loop remediation achieves 96.9% convergence versus 49.1% for block-and-retry, though the highest-convergence method reduces utility by 22.3%; feedback-driven self-repair achieves 86.6% convergence on fixable dimensions with no significant utility loss (p = 0.177). Pre-tool-call evaluation reduces unsafe agent executions by 33% (p = 0.007) with zero impact on task completion. We identify a key distinction between fixable dimensions that respond to remediation and structural dimensions (Transparency at 93.0%, Accountability at 92.8%, and Inclusivity at 82.5% failure) that require architectural rather than algorithmic solutions. The system is available as open-source SDKs.

</details>


### [32] [FST.ai 2.5: Explainable and Uncertainty-Aware AI for Olympic and Para-Taekwondo Decision Support, Athlete Digital Twins, and Federation-Scale Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16597)
*Keivan Shariatmadar,Ahmad Osman,Ramin Rey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FST·ai 2.5，一个面向奥林匹克和残奥跆拳道的可解释、不确定性感知且安全的AI框架，通过统一数字生态系统整合运动员智能、比赛分析、数字孪生和自适应训练推荐，支持世界跆拳道联合会、国家协会、教练、裁判和运动员等多元利益相关者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精英体育快速数字化带来了AI、性能分析和决策支持系统的新机遇，但现有解决方案碎片化，仅处理孤立任务（如性能分析、运动员监控或裁判支持），缺乏统一的、可解释且安全的综合框架。

Method: 提出一个统一数字生态系统，集成运动员智能、比赛分析、联合会级数据管理、AI辅助决策支持、运动员与赛事数字孪生、可解释性能指标和自适应训练推荐，采用透明、安全且联合会感知的治理机制，结合多源比赛数据、运动员表现信息和上下文证据。

Result: 原型部署证明了所提框架的可行性，提供了战术诊断、纵向运动员监控、表现预测、个性化发展规划和联合会级基准测试等功能。

Conclusion: 虽然专为奥林匹克和残奥跆拳道开发，但该方法论可广泛适用于格斗运动及其他高性能体育环境中的可解释AI、数字孪生和可信决策支持。

Abstract: The rapid digitalisation of elite sport has created new opportunities for integrating artificial intelligence (AI), performance analytics, and decision-support systems into athlete development and competition management. However, existing solutions remain fragmented, typically addressing isolated tasks such as performance analysis, athlete monitoring, or referee support. This paper presents \textbf{FST$\cdot$ai~2.5}, an explainable, uncertainty-aware, and secure AI framework for Olympic and Para-Taekwondo.
  \textbf{FST$\cdot$ai~2.5} introduces a unified digital ecosystem integrating athlete intelligence, competition analytics, federation-scale data management, AI-assisted decision support, athlete and event digital twins, explainable performance indicators, and adaptive training recommendations. The framework supports World Taekwondo (WT), Member National Associations (MNAs), coaches, referees, analysts, and athletes through transparent, secure, and federation-aware governance.
  By combining multi-source competition data, athlete-performance information, and contextual evidence, \textbf{FST$\cdot$ai~2.5} provides tactical diagnostics, longitudinal athlete monitoring, performance forecasting, personalised development planning, and federation-wide benchmarking using explainable and uncertainty-aware AI. Prototype deployments demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed framework. Although developed for Olympic and Para-Taekwondo, the methodology is broadly applicable to explainable AI, digital twins, and trustworthy decision support in combat sports and other high-performance sporting environments.

</details>


### [33] [Just A Rather Very Intelligent Spoken Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16610)
*Chen Chen,Zhehuai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JarvisBench基准，用于评估长时域AI代理工作流中实时语音中介层的价值。该中介层类似Jarvis，支持用户与代理之间的持续实时交互，包含代理协作和用户交互两个评估维度。在34个WildClaw任务上的初步实验表明，该方法能提供基于执行轨迹的问答响应，并在适当时机注入稀疏用户指导时提升任务性能，但效果高度依赖中介层的LLM能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长时域AI代理与用户的交互过于薄弱，用户仅在初始阶段给出指令，之后只能收到选择性文本更新，缺乏对代理行为的清晰感知和适时介入的能力。现有生态缺少一个始终在线的Jarvis式中介层，使得代理对用户持续可达。

Method: 提出了JarvisBench基准，包含两个互补评估轨道：(1) 代理协作轨道——衡量中介是否提升下游任务完成度；(2) 用户交互轨道——衡量中介是否让执行过程更可理解、响应更快、更易访问。同时构建了模块化参考原型Jarvis，在34个WildClaw任务上使用GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini和GPT基代理进行评估。

Result: Jarvis式中介层能够为用户问题提供基于执行轨迹的响应，并在适当时刻注入稀疏用户指导时提升任务性能。但效果强烈依赖于中介层的LLM能力。

Conclusion: Jarvis式中介层在长时域代理工作流中具有前景，填补了当前代理生态中缺失的中间层，但需要更广泛的社区努力来提升其有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon AI agents are becoming increasingly capable, yet their interaction with users remains surprisingly thin. In most workflows, users give an initial instruction, receive only selective textual updates, and lose a clear sense of what the agent is doing or when to step in. This leaves a missing part in the current agent ecosystem: an always-on Jarvis-style mediator that keeps the agent continuously reachable to the user. Such a mediator should support real-time spoken interaction with the user, answer questions without interrupting the worker, proactively report progress or confusion, and inject user guidance back into the agent's execution when useful. In this work, we introduce JarvisBench, a benchmark for measuring the dual value of mediation in long-horizon agent workflows. JarvisBench contains two complementary tracks: an agent-collaboration track that measures whether mediation improves downstream task completion, and a user-interaction track that measures whether mediation makes ongoing execution more understandable, responsive, and accessible to users. We instantiate the benchmark with a modular reference Jarvis prototype and evaluate it on 34 text-only WildClaw tasks executed in OpenClaw. Preliminary results with GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini-based, and GPT-based worker agents suggest that Jarvis-style mediation can provide trace-grounded responses to user questions and improve task performance when sparse user guidance is injected at appropriate moments. The results also show that effectiveness depends strongly on the mediator's LLM brain, highlighting both the promise of this missing middle layer and the need for broader community effort. Demo page https://cchen1436.github.io/jarvis

</details>


### [34] [A Research Prototype for Closed-Loop Generative Design of Customized Foot Orthoses via Semantic-Physics Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16631)
*Rui Wang,Byungwon Min,Suxing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TANS-FO是一个模块化闭环管道，通过文本对齐神经代理（TANS）将临床文本指令映射为3D晶格密度场，结合图神经网络（GNN）替代有限元分析实时预测足底应力，在PicoFoot-5K数据库上实现定制化足部矫形器的自动化设计与制造，达到R²=0.94的应力预测精度与34.7%的峰值压力降低，但临床数据仅为初步观察性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化临床处方与患者特异性足部矫形器（FO）3D几何参数之间存在语义-物理错配，现有设计流程依赖人工经验且缺乏即时生物力学验证，亟需一种可自动化、闭环反馈的计算设计方法。

Method: 提出TANS-FO模块化管道：1）Text-Aligned Neural Surrogate (TANS) 使用交叉注意力将临床文本嵌入投影到连续晶格密度场；2）GNN代理实时预测足底应力替代FEA；3）基于PicoFoot-5K数据库（5,230名受试者，30+解剖参数）驱动设计。

Result: GNN代理在标准化准静态加载下与Abaqus参考求解器一致（R²=0.94）；全管道在数分钟内合成可制造晶格鞋垫；男性18-40岁队列中，代理预测峰值压力较参数化CAD降低34.7%，适配误差0.42mm；探索性观察（n=12，2周随访）显示VAS疼痛评分从6.4降至2.1。

Conclusion: TANS-FO实现了从临床文本到定制化矫形器的自动化闭环设计，在计算精度和制造效率上表现出色，但临床有效性仍待严格对照试验验证，目前仅为研究原型，非临床验证设备。

Abstract: Translating unstructured clinical prescriptions into patient-specific foot orthoses (FOs) is hindered by a semantic-physical misalignment: high-level clinical intent is not mapped deterministically onto the 3D geometric parameters of the orthosis, and existing design workflows remain dependent on manual expertise with no instantaneous biomechanical validation. We present TANS-FO, a research prototype-a modular pipeline with closed-loop feedback for computational design automation of customized FOs, not a clinically validated therapeutic device. A Text-Aligned Neural Surrogate (TANS) uses cross-attention to project clinical-text embeddings onto a continuous lattice-density field, while a Graph Neural Network (GNN) surrogate predicts plantar stress in real time as a substitute for Finite Element Analysis (FEA). The framework is anchored on the open-access PicoFoot-5K anthropometric database (5,230 subjects; 30+ anatomical parameters). Under standardized quasi-static loading, the GNN surrogate agrees with an Abaqus reference solver (R^2 = 0.94), and the full pipeline synthesizes manufacturing-ready lattice insoles within minutes. On the Male 18-40 cohort, the proposed system attains a surrogate-predicted peak-pressure reduction of 34.7% over parametric CAD, with a fit error of 0.42 mm. Separately, an exploratory feasibility observation (n = 12; 2-week follow-up; no control group) using VAS pain reporting indicates short-term comfort improvement (VAS 6.4 -> 2.1), but this data is explicitly classified as preliminary observational evidence only-not evidence of clinical efficacy.

</details>


### [35] [DS@GT ARC at eRisk 2026: Hybrid Multi-Agent LLM System with Structured Algorithmic Guidance for Conversational Depression Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16712)
*Victor Gong,David Guecha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DS@GT团队在eRisk 2026任务1中提交了会话抑郁筛查系统，通过三阶段流水线（单模型原型→多智能体架构→混合配置）对LLM角色进行访谈并生成BDI-II评分和关键症状。混合方案用开源Gemma 27B替代付费GPT-5-nano，并加入对话树、可靠性加权共识聚合和聚类插补三个算法组件。最终混合Run 3达到0.9063的ADODL，在21支队伍中排名第2，成本仅为付费基线的四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个无需直接询问敏感心理健康问题、即可通过会话方式对个体进行抑郁筛查的系统，并探索用算法监督使弱开源模型在会话访谈角色中与强专有模型竞争的可能性。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1)单模型原型；(2)多智能体架构，分离会话访谈与BDI-II评分，由协调层管理；(3)混合配置，用Gemma 27B替换GPT-5-nano作为访谈者，并加入预计算对话树、可靠性加权共识聚合（Weaver框架启发）和聚类插补三个算法组件。

Result: 混合Run 3 ADODL为0.9063，在所有完整提交中排名第3，团队整体排名第2；付费基线Run 1为0.8841；混合方案成本仅为付费基线的四分之一。

Conclusion: 在有足够算法监督的情况下，弱开源模型可以在会话访谈角色中与强专有模型竞争。

Abstract: We describe DS@GT's submission to the eRisk 2026 Task 1 challenge on conversational depression screening, in which systems interview LLM personas that simulate individuals with varying depression profiles and produce a Beck Depression Inventory II (BDI-II) score plus four key symptoms per persona, without directly asking sensitive mental health questions. Our pipeline evolved through three stages: a monolithic single-model prototype to start off, a baseline multi-agent architecture that separates conversational interviewing from BDI-II scoring under a coordinating orchestration layer, and a final hybrid configuration that replaces the paid GPT-5-nano interviewer with the open-source Gemma 27B. To offset the model's weaker reasoning and instruction-following, the hybrid adds three algorithmic components: a precomputed dialogue tree that standardizes interview openers and follow-ups, a reliability-weighted consensus aggregation inspired by the Weaver framework, and a cluster-based imputation step for unprobed symptoms. We submitted three fully automated runs across all 20 personas, with Run 1 from the paid baseline and Runs 2 and 3 from the hybrid. Hybrid Run 3 achieved an ADODL of 0.9063, ranking 3rd among all complete-submission runs and placing DS@GT 2nd among the 21 teams overall, while outperforming our paid baseline Run 1 (0.8841) at roughly one-quarter of the per-persona API cost. These results support our central hypothesis that with sufficient algorithmic supervision, a weaker open-source model can compete with a stronger proprietary model in the conversational interviewer role. Our source code is available at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/erisk-task1-2026.

</details>


### [36] [RECON: Benchmarking Agent Memory for Compositional Reasoning over Long Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16716)
*Mihir Shriniwas Arya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECON是一个用于评估大语言模型在长上下文中进行组合推理能力的基准测试，涵盖三个领域（刑事、医疗、金融）的24个案卷（每份5-10万token），包含六类记忆密集型任务。评估显示，即使最强系统准确率也仅为22.4%，检索与推理均构成显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准仅评估智能体检索分散事实或检测事实是否变更的能力，但未评估事实变更后智能体能否追踪哪些下游结论受影响、哪些因独立支持而存活、以及替代时间线如何展开。RECON旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了包含24个案卷的基准RECON，覆盖刑事、医疗、金融三个领域，每份案卷50k-100k tokens。设计了六类记忆密集型任务：重建多跳证据链、传播级联失效、解决源冲突、反事实推理、满足时间约束及时间事实检索。

Result: 最强非Oracle系统准确率仅22.4%，表明当前架构在长上下文组合推理方面存在重大局限，检索与推理各自构成挑战。

Conclusion: 现有大语言模型及基于LLM的智能体在需要跨长上下文进行组合推理的记忆密集型任务上表现严重不足，RECON揭示了检索和推理两方面都是瓶颈，为未来改进方向提供了参考。

Abstract: Large language models and LLM-based agents are widely used as personal chat assistants, enterprise copilots, and autonomous workflow agents. In all these applications, memory (the ability to retain, access, and reason over information accumulated over long contexts and multiple interactions) plays a crucial role in determining the reliability of any agent. We introduce RECON (Reasoning over Extended Contexts with Obfuscated Narratives), a benchmark for evaluating compositional reasoning over long contexts. RECON spans 24 case files across three domains (criminal, medical, and financial), each ranging from 50k to 100k tokens, and tests agents on six memory intensive tasks: reconstructing multi-hop evidence chains, propagating cascading invalidations, resolving source conflicts, counterfactual reasoning, satisfying temporal constraints, and temporal fact retrieval. Recent memory benchmarks evaluate whether agents can retrieve scattered facts or detect if a fact has changed whereas RECON evaluates what happens after the change, whether agents can trace which downstream conclusions are affected, which survive through independent support, and how alternative timelines would have unfolded. Our evaluation reveals substantial limitations across current architectures: even the strongest non-Oracle system reaches only 22.4% Accuracy, with retrieval and reasoning each surfacing as challenges.

</details>


### [37] [RELIC: Revealed Principles for Learning Interpretable Composable Skills in Multi-Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16745)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Bui Dinh Pham,Duong Quoc Chinh,Dao Van Tung,Tran Cong Dao,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RELIC是一个框架，通过将行为抽象为可移植原则而非共享代码，在保护隐私的前提下实现异构多智能体间的技能学习与协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖集中式优化、共享策略或共同技能表示，无法支持隐私约束下由独立开发、接口和能力各异的智能体之间的协调。

Method: 每个智能体通过私有LLM引导搜索优化自身程序化技能；可信协调者仅基于团队整体表现评估更新；成功行为被抽象为可移植原则，供其他智能体在其自身接口中实例化并与本地策略重组。

Result: 实现了异构技能签名下的跨智能体迁移，将协调与实现分享分离，支持隐私保护下的技能学习与协调。

Conclusion: RELIC提出了隐私保护多智能体规划中技能学习与协调的新范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning becomes substantially harder when agents must improve specialized decision-making skills while keeping their internal implementations private. This regime arises when agents are developed independently, expose different interfaces and capabilities, and must nevertheless coordinate without sharing executable policies. Prior research has largely assumed centralized optimization, shared policy access, or common skill representations, making it poorly suited to privacy-constrained cooperation. We introduce RELIC, a framework for learning interpretable and composable skills via revealed principles. Each agent refines its own programmatic skill through private LLM-guided search, while a trusted orchestrator evaluates proposed updates solely through team-level performance. Successful behaviors are not broadcast as code; instead, they are abstracted into portable principles that other agents can instantiate within their own interfaces and recombine with local strategies. This separates coordination from implementation sharing, enabling cross-agent transfer under heterogeneous skill signatures. RELIC thus introduces a new paradigm for privacy-preserving skill learning and coordination in multi-agent planning.

</details>


### [38] [AgentBrew: Lifelong Knowledge Brewing from Strong Teachers to Weak LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16851)
*Yangqin Jiang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentBrew方法，通过失败触发的教师循环和学生感知合成，将教师的交互经验蒸馏为持久外部记忆，无需权重更新、专家演示或测试时教师访问，显著提升弱学生模型在编码、数学和工具使用任务上的部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署LLM智能体通常需要轻量级测试时学生模型，但存在两个核心挑战：(1) 环境仅提供稀疏的二元反馈，难以有效指导学习；(2) 教师编写的笔记必须适配更弱的学生模型，使其能够具体执行。现有方法依赖权重更新、专家演示或测试时教师访问，限制了实际部署场景。

Method: 提出AgentBrew，包含两个耦合组件：(1) Ralph Loop——失败触发的教师机制，通过将学生失败转化为环境验证的笔记来缓解稀疏反馈问题；(2) 学生感知合成——将教师知识校准到弱执行器的操作粒度上，生成模型特定、可执行的指导。

Result: 在编码、数学和工具使用任务上的大量评估和全面消融实验表明，这种非对称、免训练的酿造范式能够产生能力强大且易于部署的LLM智能体。

Conclusion: 知识酿造（Knowledge Brewing）通过将教师交互经验蒸馏为外部持久记忆，无需权重更新、专家演示、真实标签或测试时教师访问，即可有效提升弱学生模型性能，为LLM智能体部署提供了一种实用的训练范式。

Abstract: Deploying LLM agents typically requires a compact test-time student, even if a stronger teacher is available during training. We study knowledge brewing: distilling a teacher's interactive experience into a persistent external memory for the student. Crucially, this requires no weight updates, expert demonstrations, ground-truth labels, or test-time teacher access. This setting poses two challenges: environments provide only sparse, binary feedback, and teacher-authored notes must be inherently tailored to be concretely executable by a substantially weaker student. To address these hurdles, we propose AgentBrew, comprising two coupled components. First, a failure-triggered teacher--Ralph Loop mitigates sparse feedback by transforming student failures into environment-validated notes. Second, student-aware synthesis calibrates teacher knowledge to the weak executor's operational granularity, yielding model-specific, actionable guidance. Extensive evaluations and comprehensive ablations across coding, math, and tool-use tasks demonstrate that this asymmetric, training-free brewing paradigm produces highly capable yet deployable LLM agents.

</details>


### [39] [Beyond Semantic Equivalence: Logical Graphs for LLM Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16868)
*Yanni Dong,Minghua Liu,Meiling Zhu,Xiaowei Huang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出逻辑图不确定性（LGU），显式建模答案之间的蕴含和不兼容关系，在不确定性估计上优于语义熵等方法，AUROC最高提升7.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性度量（如语义熵）仅关注语义等价层面的答案一致性，忽略了不同答案间的逻辑关系（如蕴含、粒度差异），导致在逻辑兼容但形式多样的生成结果中高估不确定性、误判幻觉，需要一种能捕捉逻辑结构的度量方法。

Method: 提出Logical Graph Uncertainty (LGU)，构建答案间的逻辑蕴含图，沿蕴含链聚合概率质量，计算逻辑极大假设上的熵，并惩罚假设间的互不兼容性，从而更准确地估计不确定性。

Result: 在多个问答基准上，LGU一致优于现有方法，相对于语义熵基线，AUROC提升高达7.1%，AUARC提升高达3.5%。

Conclusion: 显式建模答案间的逻辑关系可以显著提升不确定性估计的准确性，减少对逻辑兼容但形式多样化的生成结果的误判，有助于提升LLM在安全敏感场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often produce confidently stated yet unreliable outputs, posing critical challenges for deployment in safety-sensitive applications. Existing uncertainty metrics such as semantic entropy capture agreement at the level of semantic equivalence, but largely ignore the logical relationships between distinct answers. As a result, they tend to overestimate uncertainty and falsely flag hallucinations in settings where generated responses are diverse in form yet logically compatible (e.g., differing only in granularity or specificity). We propose Logical Graph Uncertainty (LGU), a framework that explicitly models implication and incompatibility among answers. LGU aggregates probability mass along entailment chains, computes entropy over logically maximal hypotheses, and penalizes mutual incompatibility among them. Across multiple question-answering benchmarks, LGU consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing methods, and outperforms the semantic entropy baseline by up to +7.1% AUROC and +3.5% AUARC across datasets.

</details>


### [40] [Environment-free Synthetic Data Generation for API-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16900)
*Seanie Lee,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Chao Jiang,Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Ting-Yao Hu,Alexander T Toshev,Oncel Tuzel,Raviteja Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需可执行环境的合成数据生成方法，利用大语言模型作为即时数字世界模型，仅基于API规范即可生成高质量的API调用代理训练轨迹，在AppWorld和OfficeBench基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练API调用型LLM代理需要大量高质量轨迹数据，但传统方法依赖完整实现的可执行环境和预填充后端数据库，这成为规模化扩展的主要瓶颈。
