No model consistently outperforms PyTorch when evaluated against realistic baselines. On the memory front, 28% of GPU kernels generated by the best model increase peak GPU memory usage. Our findings demonstrate the necessity of continually adapting robust evaluation protocols as LLM kernel generation capabilities advance.

</details>


### [75] [TRACE: Trajectory-Based Safety Patch Learning for LLM Post-Training Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16242)
*Changyue Li,Jiaming He,Youliang Yuan,Jialin Wu,Boxi Yu,Zhicong Huang,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于轨迹的安全补丁学习框架，通过在模拟有害微调轨迹上优化插件式安全补丁，在几乎不影响下游任务效用的前提下恢复模型安全性，在六个基准和两个模型上达到近100%安全率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTaaS平台中微调会破坏模型的安全对齐，现有基于参数合并的安全修复方法受限于任务-安全更新纠缠问题：安全向量缩放过弱无法抑制有害成分，缩放过强则抑制任务相关方向，导致安全与效用难以平衡。

Method: 提出TRACE框架：(i) 模拟有害微调轨迹，生成逐渐被破坏的状态；(ii) 优化一个插件式安全补丁，使其在不同被破坏的基础状态下都能恢复安全性同时保持效用，从而将焦点从在线合并操作转向离线补丁学习。

Result: 在六个基准和两个模型上，TRACE在所有设置下达到近100%安全率，同时保持与未防御的微调模型相当的效用，持续占据安全-效用前沿。

Conclusion: 通过离线补丁学习替代在线参数合并，TRACE有效解决了任务-安全更新纠缠问题，实现了安全恢复与效用保持的最佳权衡。

Abstract: Fine-Tuning-as-a-Service (FTaaS) platforms let users train large language models (LLMs) on customized tasks, but this pipeline could erode models' safety alignment. In practice, service providers need to recover models' safety without re-running full alignment, or destroying the utility gained from customized tasks. A line of existing work refers to model parameter merging, which adds a safety patch on the fine-tuned model parameters to shift the model away from unsafe tendencies. However, this merging-based paradigm is fundamentally bottlenecked by task-safety update entanglement: downstream task updates and the safety patch often overlap in their dominant directions, so the merge strength is intrinsically hard to calibrate. If the safety vector is scaled too weakly, harmful components could still dominate, preventing the model from returning to a safe region; if it is scaled too aggressively, it suppresses task-relevant directions and degrades utility.
  To solve this problem, we shift the focus of merging-based methods from designing online merging operators to offline patch learning, and seek a safety patch that minimally interferes with task-relevant directions while retaining decisive control over unsafe behaviors. We propose TRACE, a trajectory-based safety patch learning framework that (i) simulates harmful tuning trajectories to generate progressively corrupted states, and (ii) optimizes a plug-in patch to recover safety while maintaining utility across varying corrupted base states.
  Across six benchmarks and two models, TRACE consistently dominates the safety-utility frontier. TRACE reaches nearly 100% safety on all settings, while maintaining comparable utility to the undefended fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [76] [CIGPO: Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Evidence-Reading LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16244)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIGPO，通过注入每轮信息增益（IG）奖励来防止GRPO在多轮证据阅读任务中的零优势锁死（reward-variance collapse），在HotpotQA上将F1从0.252提升至0.518，而标准GRPO最终完全崩溃至0.000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮证据阅读智能体训练中，仅使用结果奖励的强化学习（如GRPO）存在中间轮次缺乏直接信用分配的问题，导致训练不稳定甚至崩溃。作者诊断出"零优势锁死"机制：所有采样轨迹均获得最低格式惩罚，组内相对优势消失，策略梯度变为零，形成优化死锁。

Method: 提出Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization (CIGPO)，核心策略是注入方差：将冻结参考模型在真实答案上的对数似然边际增量作为每轮信息增益（IG）奖励，与F1奖励分别归一化，并采用IG权重课程学习。通过赋予中间证据阅读轮次独立奖励，防止组奖励分布坍缩为单一值，从而维持GRPO所需的组内相对优势差异。

Result: 在HotpotQA上，CIGPO在3B规模下达到标准F1 0.518（基线0.252，+105%），而最佳GRPO检查点仅0.430，最终GRPO检查点完全崩溃至0.000。CIGPO在整个训练过程中保持有意义的奖励方差，避免了零优势锁死。

Conclusion: 识别出奖励方差坍缩是仅使用结果奖励的GRPO的一个具体失败模式，并证明在HotpotQA场景下，引入轮级信息增益奖励可以有效防止该问题。CIGPO通过保持奖励分布的多样性，确保了GRPO组内相对优势机制的正常运作。

Abstract: Training multi-turn evidence-reading agents with outcome-only reinforcement learning is unstable because intermediate turns receive little direct credit. In HotpotQA experiments with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, GRPO initially improves (standard F1 0.430) but subsequently collapses to 100% format-violating outputs. Training-log diagnosis reveals a zero-advantage lock-in mechanism: all sampled trajectories receive the minimum format penalty (-2.0), group-relative advantages vanish, and the policy-gradient loss becomes zero--an optimization deadlock. We propose a variance-injection strategy: by assigning per-turn rewards to intermediate evidence-reading turns, we prevent the group reward distribution from collapsing to a single value--preserving the variation that GRPO's group-relative advantage requires. Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization (CIGPO) implements this strategy using the marginal increase in the frozen reference model's log-likelihood of the ground-truth answer as the per-turn signal. With separate normalization of IG and F1 rewards and an IG-weight curriculum, CIGPO reaches a standard F1 of 0.518 on HotpotQA at the 3B scale (from 0.252 base; +105%), compared with 0.430 for the best GRPO checkpoint and 0.000 for the final GRPO checkpoint. CIGPO maintains meaningful reward variance and avoids zero-advantage lock-in throughout training. These results identify reward-variance collapse as a concrete failure mode of outcome-only GRPO and show that turn-level IG rewards can prevent it in this HotpotQA setting.

</details>


### [77] [Let the Data Decide: Supervision Analysis, Capability Trade-offs, and Adaptive Objective Routing in Continued Pre-Training via Off-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16246)
*Jiangan Yuan,Zhixuan Li,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大规模语言模型预训练中的离策略蒸馏问题，将训练目标与模型能力的关系分解为"目标→能力"和"数据→目标"两个层面。研究发现语言建模目标（LLM）和知识蒸馏目标（LKD）会产生不同的能力特征，其根源在于梯度层面的直接观测token强化与教师支持的备选监督之间的张力。通过引入支持覆盖率、观测token概率质量、教师分布集中度等诊断指标，发现支持大小k控制覆盖-锐度权衡，而蒸馏温度调节支持内的概率分配。更重要的是，在域级别的自适应目标路由中，对数学和代码使用LLM、对通用数据使用LKD的策略一致优于单目标基线，而token级别的路由无法稳定超越基线。这表明有效目标路由的关键在于路由信号质量而非粒度，从而将离策略蒸馏下的继续预训练重新定义为结构化、数据条件化的监督设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前离策略蒸馏在大型语言模型预训练中占据核心地位，但训练数据、目标参数化与模型能力之间的相互作用机制尚未被充分刻画。现有研究缺乏对客观到能力以及数据到目标这两个关键映射的系统分析，导致无法有效指导目标路由策略的设计。

Method: 将问题分解为"目标→能力"和"数据→目标"两个分析维度。首先通过实验对比LLM和LKD在不同配置下的能力特征差异，引入支持覆盖率、观测token概率质量、教师分布集中度等诊断指标量化梯度层面的张力。然后系统对比域级别和token级别的自适应目标路由策略。

Result: LLM和LKD产生不同的能力特征，其差异源于梯度层面的直接观测token强化与教师支持的备选监督之间的张力。支持大小k控制覆盖-锐度权衡，蒸馏温度调节支持内的概率分配。域级别的自适应路由（LLM用于数学/代码，LKD用于通用数据）一致优于单目标基线，而token级别路由无法稳定超越基线。

Conclusion: 有效目标路由更依赖于路由信号质量而非粒度，离策略蒸馏下的继续预训练应被视为结构化、数据条件化的监督设计问题，而非全局超参数选择问题。

Abstract: Off-policy distillation is now central to large language model pre-training, yet how training data, objective parameterization, and model capabilities interact remains poorly characterized. We studies top-$k$-truncated, temperature-scaled off-policy distillation by decomposing this problem into two questions: an \emph{objective-to-capability} analysis of how the training objective shapes token-level supervision and downstream performance, and a \emph{data-to-objective} analysis of how data heterogeneity should inform objective routing. We first show that the language-modeling objective ($L_{\mathrm{LM}}$) and the knowledge-distillation objective ($L_{\mathrm{KD}}$) induce systematically different capability profiles, and trace this divergence to a gradient-level tension between \emph{direct observed-token reinforcement} and \emph{teacher-supported alternative supervision}. To quantify this tension, we introduce diagnostic metrics -- support coverage, observed-token probability mass, and teacher-distribution concentration -- and show via controlled sweeps that the support size $k$ governs a coverage-sharpness trade-off, while distillation temperature controls within-support probability allocation. We then examine adaptive objective routing: a domain-level policy that applies $L_{\mathrm{LM}}$ to math and code and $L_{\mathrm{KD}}$ to general-domain data yields consistent gains over both single-objective baselines, whereas token-level routing based on observed-token probability mass or teacher entropy fails to consistently match the single-objective baseline. These results suggest that effective objective routing depends less on routing granularity than on the quality of the routing signal, reframing continued pre-training via off-policy distillation as a structured, data-conditional supervision-design problem rather than a global hyperparameter choice.

</details>


### [78] [High-accuracy Low-Bit KV-Cache Quantization via Local Distribution Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16248)
*Gradwell Dzikanyanga,Yanqi Pan,Weihao Yang,Donglei Wu,Wen Xia,Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现低比特KV缓存量化导致长上下文LLM推理质量下降的根本原因不是logits的绝对误差，而是结构化局部排序错误（top-K区域logits分布漂移）。为此提出局部分布恢复技术DGAP，通过检测高风险步骤并选择性恢复top-K候选分布，在Llama-3.1-8B上将1-bit量化RULER准确率从47.8%恢复至83.2%，分布漂移从0.38降至0.14，且保持低比特KV缓存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存带来高内存和带宽开销，低比特量化可降低成本但严重损害质量（如1-bit量化准确率从84.2%降至47.8%）。现有观点认为是logits绝对误差导致的，但本文发现真正原因是结构化局部排序错误。

Method: 提出局部分布恢复技术（DGAP）：从量化logits特征中检测高局部分布风险的步骤，然后仅对选中的top-K候选分布进行恢复，包含高效的风险检测器和校正器。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B上，DGAP将K1V1 RULER准确率从47.8%恢复至83.2%，分布漂移从0.38降至0.14；在Llama、Mistral、Qwen等多个模型上均保持低比特KV缓存占用，解码开销适度。

Conclusion: 低比特KV缓存量化的质量下降源于局部排序错误而非绝对误差，通过局部分布恢复可显著缓解，为实现高效长上下文推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference relies on the KV cache to avoid redundant attention computation, but incurs high memory and bandwidth overheads. Low-bit KV-cache quantization reduces this cost, yet it severely degrade quality; particularly, one-bit quantization reduces accuracy from 84.2% to 47.8% on Llama-3.1-8B under RULER.
  Rather than common beliefs that absolute error of logits, we find that the root cause is structured local misranking, where the distribution of logits in top-K region is drifted. We thereby propose local distribution restoration, a new technique that detects steps with high local distribution risk from quantized-logit features and restores only the selected top-K candidate distribution before token selection. We implement DGAP to achieve local distribution restoration, with efficient risk detcetors and correctors. Expeirments show that on Llama-3.1-8B, DGAP recovers K1V1 RULER accuracy from 47.8% to 83.2% and reduces distribution drift from 0.38 to 0.14; across Llama, Mistral, and Qwen models, it preserves the persistent low-bit KV-cache footprint with modest decode overhead.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models from Pure Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16251)
*Yutong Feng,Shiyuan Piao,Yutong Xia,Xu Liu,Wenqi Fan,Fugee Tsung,See-Kiong Ng,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeoST是首个完全基于程序生成的合成数据预训练的时空基础模型，通过潜在空间推理架构、合成预训练语料库和潜在空间目标函数，解决了现有STFM在真实数据分布偏差、自回归/扩散模型结构瓶颈和点式重建目标过度依赖噪声观测等方面的问题，在多个真实世界基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空基础模型面临三个主要问题：真实世界预训练数据存在分布偏差、自回归或扩散范式存在结构瓶颈、目标函数在噪声观测空间中过度强调点式重建。

Method: 提出NeoST，包含三个核心设计：1）可扩展的合成预训练语料库（纯程序生成的合成系统）；2）潜在空间推理架构（生成并迭代精炼多条未来轨迹，避免顺序误差累积）；3）潜在空间目标函数（强调结构动力学，支持分布偏移下的推理时校正）。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准测试中，NeoST在多样化的时空系统上持续优于现有STFM，展现出更优的长时域稳定性和推理效率。

Conclusion: 基于合成数据预训练的潜在空间推理方法可以有效克服真实数据偏差和模型结构限制，为构建更通用的时空基础模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models (STFMs) aim to learn generalizable representations of complex dynamical systems across space and time. However, existing approaches suffer from distributional bias in real-world pre-training data, structural bottlenecks of autoregressive or diffusion-based paradigms, and objectives that overemphasize point-wise reconstruction in noisy observation space.We propose \textbf{NeoST}, the first spatio-temporal foundation model pre-trained solely on procedurally generated synthetic systems. NeoST introduces a scalable synthetic pre-training corpus to mitigate real-world bias, a latent-space reasoning architecture that generates and iteratively refines multiple future trajectories without sequential error accumulation, and latent-space objectives that emphasize structural dynamics and enable inference-time correction under distribution shifts.Extensive experiments across diverse real-world benchmarks show that NeoST consistently outperforms existing STFMs in diverse real-world spatio-temporal systems, achieves superior long-horizon stability and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [80] [Feature Generation Using LLMs: An Evolutionary Algorithm Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16255)
*Aria Nourbakhsh,Benoît Alcaraz,Christoph Schommer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）从表格数据的已有特征中自动生成新特征的流程，并通过选择算法挑选最优特征集，实验表明该方法在多个数据集上能有效提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征工程是机器学习流程中的关键步骤，但传统方法依赖人工经验，耗时且难以发现复杂特征关系。利用LLM在数学推理和世界知识方面的能力，自动生成有意义的特征。

Method: 构建一个pipeline：输入已有特征集和提示词，LLM基于数学和逻辑运算生成新特征；然后通过选择算法评估并挑选性能最优的特征子集。

Result: 在8个不同领域和数据类型的数据集上，LLM生成的新特征在多数情况下能提升分类任务的表现。

Conclusion: LLM能够基于数学和逻辑运算符生成对分类任务有用的新特征，是一种有效的自动特征工程方法。

Abstract: A crucial step in machine learning pipelines is to present each entity with features or attributes that are representative of the characteristics of the processed entities. Feature engineering is an important step in finding a relation among attributes that otherwise may not be processed by the ML algorithms. Meanwhile, Large Language Models have shown promising abilities in coding, mathematical reasoning, and processing world knowledge. In this work, we utilize an LLM for the problem of feature generation from tabular data based on the previously given features. We have created a pipeline that takes a set of attributes and a prompt to generate new features. Then, our selection algorithm selects the best-performing sets of attributes. We apply our method to eight datasets from different domains and data types. Our results show that, in most cases, the language model can produce new features based on mathematical and logical operators that are useful for the given tasks and can improve classification results.

</details>


### [81] [Quantifying Ranking Uncertainty in LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16259)
*Bitya Neuhof,Yuval Benjamini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了MMLU基准测试中的排名不确定性来源，并提出改进的假设检验方法来量化这些不确定性，证明MMLU各学科间的排名变异性很大，应在比较LLMs或识别最佳模型时予以考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务排行榜上预训练模型的排名存在不确定性，近期虽然引入了排名置信区间方法来量化这种不确定性，但现有方法未能充分考虑知识评估基准中的特殊不确定性来源，因此需要深入分析这些来源并改进假设检验方法。

Method: 通过分析MMLU基准测试中知识评估的不确定性来源，对原有的基于成对假设检验聚合的排名置信区间方法进行修改，以纳入这些效应的影响。

Result: 发现MMLU各学科间的排名变异性显著，这种变异性应该被纳入LLMs比较和最佳模型识别的考量中。

Conclusion: 在比较LLMs或识别最佳模型时，必须考虑MMLU各学科间的排名变异性，这对多任务排行榜的可靠性有重要影响。

Abstract: Pretrained models are typically ranked on multi-task leaderboards to assess their effectiveness across diverse tasks. Rank confidence intervals were recently introduced as a method to quantify the uncertainty in these rankings by aggregating pairwise hypothesis tests. In this work, we analyze the sources of uncertainty in the knowledge evaluation benchmark MMLU and show how hypothesis tests can be modified to account for their effects. We demonstrate that ranking variability across MMLU subjects is substantial and should be considered when comparing LLMs or identifying the top-performing models.

</details>


### [82] [Autonomous mechanistic discovery of colorectal cancer vulnerabilities via multi-scale AI swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16262)
*Christopher Baker,Tianyu Ren,Karen Rafferty,Hui Wang,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Octopus（多尺度自主发现引擎）的神经符号架构，该架构将零泄露的本地LLM群与严格的算法物理引擎相结合，实现了从体外CRISPR筛选到体内肿瘤轨迹预测及人类总生存期预测的全自动、端到端的生物医学发现。在结直肠癌转录组的无监督扫描中，它自主识别出IGF2为5-氟尿嘧啶耐药的严格边界脆弱性，并经过严格统计验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体框架虽能自主生成假设和进行体外实验分析，但缺乏数学基础的因果约束，无法实现多尺度临床转化；而算法临床数字孪生虽能预测生物状态，但依赖黑箱潜在空间，牺牲了机械可解释性。这两者之间的认知鸿沟阻碍了自动化科学发现的加速。

Method: Octopus架构包含三个核心组件：1）零泄露本地LLM群用于自主生成治疗假设；2）严格算法物理引擎提供数学约束的因果动力学；3）机械可解释性模块（XGBoost SHAP向量）追踪动态因果级联。系统在CCLE体外CRISPR数据上生成假设，通过机械可解释性追踪因果级联，然后在硅片上将涌现的脆弱性正交转化为体内预测（PDX小鼠模型和Marisa人类总生存期数据）。

Result: 在结直肠癌转录组的全自动无监督扫描中，系统自主识别出IGF2为5-FU耐药的严格边界脆弱性。该发现经Benjamini-Hochberg FDR校正后仍显著（q=0.0292, Log-Rank p=0.0007），并在独立小鼠队列中成功预测了显著的体内肿瘤体积缩小（Mann-Whitney p=0.0373）。

Conclusion: Octopus架构弥合了多智能体推理与数学约束的临床生存之间的鸿沟，建立了一种可验证、零泄露的端到端自动生物医学发现范式，为自动化科学发现提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: The acceleration of automated scientific discovery has been fundamentally bottlenecked by the epistemic gap between the semantic reasoning of large language models (LLMs) and the deterministic physics of mammalian biology. While recent multi-agent frameworks have achieved autonomous hypothesis generation and in vitro experimental analysis, they lack the mathematically grounded, causal constraints required for multi-scale clinical translation. Furthermore, while algorithmic clinical digital twins successfully forecast biological states, they rely on black-box latent spaces, sacrificing mechanistic interpretability for predictive accuracy. Here, we introduce the Multi-Scale Autonomous Discovery Engine (Octopus), a neuro-symbolic architecture that unites zero-leakage, local LLM swarms with strict algorithmic physics engines. Rather than stopping at isolated cellular assays, the system autonomously generated therapeutic hypotheses against in vitro CRISPR dependency data (CCLE), traced dynamic causal cascades using mechanistic interpretability (XGBoost SHAP vectors), and orthogonally translated the emergent vulnerabilities in silico to predict in vivo mammalian tumor trajectory (PDX) and human overall survival (Marisa). In a fully unsupervised sweep of colorectal cancer transcriptomes, the pipeline autonomously identified Insulin-like Growth Factor 2 (IGF2) as a strictly bounded vulnerability to 5-Fluorouracil resistance. The discovery maintained significance after rigorous Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate correction (q=0.0292, Log-Rank p=0.0007 ) and successfully predicted significant in vivo tumor volume shrinkage in an independent mouse cohort (Mann-Whitney p=0.0373). By bridging the chasm between multi-agent reasoning and mathematically bounded clinical survival, this framework establishes a verifiable, zero-leakage paradigm for automated, end-to-end biomedical discovery.

</details>


### [83] [Retrieval is Enough: Training-Free Interpretability with a Tool-Using Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16448)
*Sriram Balasubramanian,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARP是一种假设驱动的智能体检索与探针方法，通过LLM智能体配合向量数据库和激活操作工具，无需训练即可在多种可解释性任务上超越基于训练的方法（SAE、激活预言机），表明当前训练方法并未提取出超越训练数据的洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络激活可解释性方法存在成本谱系：从廉价的无训练方法（线性探针、PCA、SVD）到昂贵的基于训练的方法（SAE、激活预言机）。后者通常更强大，部分原因是它们在训练期间利用了大量激活数据集。这引发了一个自然问题：这些方法是否真的发现了超越训练数据集本身可恢复的洞察？

Method: HARP（Hypothesis-driven Agentic Retrieval and Probing）方法：使用一个配备了向量数据库（存储激活值及其文本上下文）和操作工具（投影去除潜在空间方向、计算激活差异和平均值）的LLM智能体。智能体迭代执行以下步骤：查询数据库→从检索样本中形成假设→通过构建线性探针验证假设。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 尽管不涉及任何训练，HARP在概念发现、概念检测、模型引导和秘密提取任务上均优于激活预言机和基于SAE的智能体。无训练设计使HARP更便宜、更灵活：当现有数据集不足时可以按需索引新数据集。

Conclusion: 当前基于训练的可解释性方法并未从激活数据中提取出超越训练数据本身的洞察；HARP作为一种无需训练的方法，在概念发现、检测、模型引导和秘密提取等任务上超越了基于训练的方法（如激活预言机和SAE智能体），且成本更低、更灵活。这提示需要设计明确要求方法展示超越训练数据洞察力的基准测试。

Abstract: Interpretability methods for neural network activations span a wide cost spectrum, from cheap, training-free techniques (such as linear probes, PCA, SVD) to more expensive training-based ones (such as SAEs and activation oracles). Training-based methods are typically more powerful, in part because they leverage large activation datasets during training. This raises a natural question - do they actually surface insights that go beyond what is recoverable from the training dataset itself? To address this, we equip an LLM agent with a vector database of activations paired with their textual contexts, along with tools for manipulating activations - projecting out directions in latent space, computing activation differences and averages. The agent iteratively queries the database, forms hypotheses from the retrieved samples, and validates them by constructing linear probes. We call this method HARP, for Hypothesis-driven Agentic Retrieval and Probing. Despite not involving any training, HARP outperforms both activation oracles and SAE-based agents on concept discovery, concept detection, model steering, and secret elicitation. The training-free design also makes HARP substantially cheaper and more flexible: new datasets can be indexed on demand whenever existing ones prove insufficient. More broadly, our results suggest that current training-based methods do not yet extract insights beyond their training data, and motivate benchmarks that explicitly require interpretability methods to demonstrate such insights. We release our code at https://github.com/SriramB-98/HARP

</details>


### [84] [Learning from World Feedback: Why Model Uncertainty Fails as a Risk Signal in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16591)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLxF纲领，主张学习信号应来自世界反馈而非内部模型代理。在安全模型预测控制中，基于动态度量的不确定性惩罚反而增加碰撞率（26%→34%），而使用三种世界反馈信号（传感器裕度、时间-碰撞信号、基于碰撞标签训练的结果监督反馈模型）可将碰撞率降至1-14%。机制在于模型不确定性与任务风险在支撑集上不相关（r<0.15），并提炼出三条RLxF原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对标准MBRL中基于模型内部不确定性的安全代理（如动态度量惩罚）在实际安全任务中表现不佳（与安全目标负相关）的问题，提出学习信号应从世界反馈而非内部模型代理获取的RLxF纲领。

Method: 1) 在四种世界模型架构上（涵盖2x MSE范围）进行MPC规划对比实验，使用TOST统计等价性检验（n=200）；2) 比较标准动态度量不确定性惩罚与三种世界反馈信号：基于激光雷达最小值的传感器裕度、基于时间-碰撞的时序信号、基于历史碰撞标签训练的结果监督反馈模型g_psi（结构与RLHF中的奖励模型类似）；3) 分析模型不确定性与任务风险的支撑集差异及相关性。

Result: 1) 四种世界模型下MPC规划统计等价（TOST检验）；2) 动态度量不确定性惩罚使碰撞率从26%升至34%，即标准安全代理与安全目标负相关；3) 世界反馈信号将碰撞率降至1-14%，无需重新训练世界模型或规划器；4) 模型不确定性与任务风险相关性r<0.15，支撑集不同。

Conclusion: 提炼三条RLxF原则：①将风险根植于世界结果而非模型内部度量；②在部署前验证代理指标的有效性；③当直接世界信号不可得时，使用结果监督反馈模型替代。这些原则同样适用于基于验证器或RLHF的LLM对齐方法。

Abstract: The RLxF programme argues that learning signals should come from world feedback rather than from internal model proxies. We instantiate this position in safe model-based control and distil it into three concrete design principles. Empirically, across four world-model architectures spanning a 2x MSE range, MPC planning is statistically equivalent (TOST, n=200), and dynamics-based uncertainty penalties increase collision rates from 26% to 34%: the standard MBRL safety proxy is anti-correlated with safety in this regime. Replacing the model-internal proxy with three world-feedback signals (a sensor-derived margin via minimum lidar, a temporal signal via time-to-collision, and an outcome-supervised feedback model g_psi trained on prior collision labels, structurally analogous to outcome-trained reward models in RLHF) reduces collisions to 1-14% without retraining the world model or the planner. The mechanism is structural: model uncertainty has support over state-prediction space, whereas task risk has support over constraint boundaries, with empirical correlation r < 0.15. From this we extract three RLxF principles (ground risk in world outcomes, validate proxies before deployment, and substitute outcome-trained feedback models when direct world signals are unavailable) and argue they apply equally to model-based control and to verifier-based or RLHF approaches in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Memory Leaderboards: Evaluating Scientific Memory as Budgeted Context Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16848)
*Maksim Sheverev,David Finkelstein,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两个科学文献记忆基准（PAIM和PTr），评估了八种记忆/检索系统，发现记忆排行榜不可解释，受摄取粒度、检索预算、模态等因素影响；提出应将科学记忆评估视为有预算的、模态感知的上下文恢复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆基准大多评估对话或摘要，而研究型智能体需要从完整科学论文中恢复证据，缺乏针对科学文献全文的记忆评估基准。

Method: 构建两个科学全文记忆基准PAIM（81篇论文，66个问题）和PTr（252篇论文，98个问题）；评估八种记忆/检索系统（包括自研的Theoria）及无检索基线；通过多裁判和人工侧对侧校准进行一致性验证。

Result: 1) 记忆排行榜不可解释，受摄取粒度、原始文本保留、检索预算、检索模态、评分准则和裁判选择影响；2) Graphiti在PAIM上获胜但每查询使用260万字符上下文，控制预算后优势消失；3) 稀疏-密集混合检索是PTr上最有效的干预手段，hybrid变体在0.03分内并列领先；4) LLM裁判排名与人工评估一致，有效分辨率约为10分制中的1分。

Conclusion: 科学记忆评估应作为有预算的、模态感知的上下文恢复任务，而非无约束的架构排行榜；发布数据集、工具和代码以支持此类评估。

Abstract: Long-term memory is becoming a core component of LLM agents, but most memory benchmarks evaluate conversations or compact summaries, while research agents need to restore evidence from full scientific papers. We introduce two full-text scientific-memory benchmarks, Public AI Memory (PAIM; 81 papers, 66 questions) and Public Transformers (PTr; 252 papers, 98 questions). We evaluate eight memory/retrieval systems, including our own proposed Theoria, plus a no-retrieval baseline. Our results show that memory leaderboards are not interpretable without the full protocol: ingestion granularity, raw-text preservation, retrieval budget, retrieval modality, rubric audit, and judge choice all affect the outcome. For example, on PAIM Graphiti wins convincingly but uses 2.6M characters of retrieved context per query, and after controlling for retrieval budget the lead disappears. On PTr, for the systems where BM25 retrieval can be added cleanly, the sparse-dense hybrid is the single most significant intervention: hybrid variants of Simple RAG, Mem0, and Theoria tie for the lead within 0.03 points. Multi-judge and human side-by-side calibration show that LLM-as-a-judge rankings are consistent across frontier judges and agree with human evaluation, with an effective resolution of roughly one point on a ten-point scale. We argue that scientific memory should be evaluated as budgeted, modality-aware context restoration rather than as an unconstrained architecture leaderboard, and we release the datasets, harness, raw outputs, judgments, and scripts to reproduce our results and serve as tools for such evaluation. Our code is available at http://gitlab.com/quantellence/research/scientific-recall-bench , and the datasets are available at http://huggingface.co/datasets/quantellence/srb-data .

</details>


### [86] [Bridging battery design and health assessment through virtual sensing and physics-informed learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16864)
*Wendi Guo,Søren Byg Vilsen,Daniel Ioan Stroe,Yaqi Li,Yicun Huang,Ashima Verma,Daniel Brandell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息学习框架，利用虚拟传感技术从标准BMS测量数据中推断难以测量的电池设计参数（如固相扩散系数、电极厚度、离子浓度和颗粒尺寸），将数字孪生衍生的颗粒开裂机制作为软约束嵌入，仅需2%早期观测数据即可将轨迹和寿命预测误差降低6-8倍，同时将容量损失误差降低39%、寿命终点误差降低17%、预测变异性降低54%，建立了部署与开发之间的实用反馈循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电池管理系统与电池老化的材料和结构根源脱节，既限制了可解释的健康评估，也阻碍了有信息依据的电池设计。急需一种能够从标准BMS测量中直接推断设计参数并建立运行与设计之间反馈的方法。

Method: 构建物理信息学习框架，将数字孪生衍生的颗粒开裂机制作为软约束嵌入模型，利用虚拟传感技术从标准BMS测量数据（如电压、电流等）推断固相扩散系数、电极厚度、离子浓度和颗粒尺寸等设计参数。

Result: 在多种快充策略和驾驶工况下，仅用2%早期观测数据，轨迹和寿命预测误差相比最先进的机器学习基线降低6-8倍；容量损失误差降低39%，EOL误差降低17%，预测变异性降低54%。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了部署与开发之间的实用反馈循环，证明了真实世界运行可以持续为上游复杂多物理系统的设计决策提供信息，且准确的老化外推无需完全解析的控制方程，验证后的部分机制结合有限数据即可提供充分指导。

Abstract: Supercharging of lithium-ion batteries (LiBs) requires robust health monitoring to ensure durability, safety, and user confidence, particularly for emerging vehicle-to-grid applications with bidirectional energy flows. Yet battery management remains largely disconnected from the material and structural origins of aging, limiting both interpretable health assessment and informed battery design. Here we propose a physics-informed learning framework with virtual sensing that infers hard-to-measure design parameters, including solid-state diffusion coefficient, electrode thickness, ion concentration, and particle size, directly from standard battery management system (BMS) measurements. Across diverse fast-charging strategies and driving profiles, embedding a digital-twin-derived particle-cracking mechanism as a soft constraint reduces trajectory and lifetime prediction errors by 6-8 times relative to state-of-the-art machine learning baselines using only 2% early-life observations. We further show that accurate degradation extrapolation does not require fully resolved governing equations; validated partial mechanisms, jointly refined with limited data, provide sufficient guidance. Virtual sensing transforms standard charging signals into latent design variables without additional sensors, bridging observable battery behavior and underlying aging processes while reducing capacity loss error by up to 39%, end-of-life (EOL) error by 17%, and prediction variability by up to 54%, enabling real-time exploration of new battery configurations. More broadly, the proposed framework establishes a practical feedback loop between deployment and development, demonstrating how real-world operation can continuously inform upstream design decisions across complex multiphysics systems.

</details>


### [87] [Enhancing Personalized Bladder Cancer Treatment Through Reinforcement Learning: A Recurrent Patient State Transition Decision Support Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16916)
*Divyansh Chawla,Anshu Garg,Isshaan Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合患者状态转移预测建模、马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）和深度Q网络（DQN）强化学习的递归患者状态转移模拟框架，用于膀胱癌治疗规划。该框架能够动态、个性化地优化治疗决策，并在模拟环境中实现了累积奖励63,918.87、平均训练损失0.0056、策略改进6.62%的性能，优于现有强化学习治疗方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的膀胱癌临床决策支持系统依赖静态治疗指南或单步预测模型，无法捕捉疾病随时间进展的动态变化，尤其对于复发型膀胱癌，治疗效果在不同临床阶段会发生变化，需要个性化和自适应的决策支持。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：（1）预测性状态转移建模模块，用于估计治疗后肿瘤特征的变化；（2）马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），对患者治疗过程中的状态转移进行形式化建模；（3）深度Q网络（DQN）强化学习环境，智能体通过与模拟患者轨迹交互，序贯优化治疗决策。框架可生成可解释的治疗轨迹和详细的模拟日志。

Result: 在模拟的复发治疗环境中，该框架累积奖励达63,918.87，每轮平均训练损失为0.0056，策略改进得分为6.62%，优于已有的基于强化学习的治疗规划方法。

Conclusion: 递归患者状态转移模拟与强化学习的结合，为个性化膀胱癌治疗规划提供了一个灵活、可解释的决策支持框架，展现了AI辅助精准肿瘤学的应用潜力。

Abstract: Bladder cancer treatment requires personalized and adaptive decision-making, particularly for recurrent disease, where treatment effectiveness changes across successive clinical episodes. Conventional clinical decision support systems typically rely on static treatment guidelines or single-step predictive models, limiting their ability to capture disease progression over time. This paper presents a recurrent patient state-transition simulation framework for bladder cancer treatment planning that integrates predictive state-transition modeling with a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and a Deep Q-Network (DQN) reinforcement learning environment. The predictive module estimates changes in tumor characteristics following treatment, while the reinforcement learning agent sequentially optimizes treatment decisions by interacting with simulated patient trajectories. This framework enables dynamic, patient-specific treatment planning by continuously adapting recommendations to evolving clinical states. It also generates interpretable treatment trajectories and detailed simulation logs to improve transparency and support clinical decision-making. The proposed framework was evaluated against existing reinforcement learning-based treatment planning approaches. It achieved a cumulative reward of 63,918.87, an average training loss per episode of 0.0056, and a policy improvement score of 6.62%, demonstrating effective sequential learning and robust treatment optimization in a simulated recurrent treatment environment. These findings highlight the potential of recurrent patient state-transition simulation with reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support framework for personalized bladder cancer treatment planning and AI-assisted precision oncology.

</details>


### [88] [SurvCF(t): Counterfactual Explanations for Survival Analysis in Predictive Maintenance Multivariate Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16969)
*Zara Karazian,Panagiotis Papapetrou,Sindri Magnússon,Erik Frisk,Tony Lindgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SurvCF(t)框架，首个为时间序列数据上的生存模型生成反事实解释的方法，通过最小化、可行且时间一致的修改来延长设备预测寿命，在多个基准数据集上验证了其生成可解释干预的能力，弥合了生存预测与处方性维护之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度生存模型在剩余寿命预测上表现优异，但其黑箱特性限制了在安全关键场景中的应用，缺乏可操作的洞察力。现有方法无法为时间序列数据上的生存模型提供反事实解释。

Method: 将反事实解释建模为约束优化问题，同时考虑有效性(预测寿命增加)、邻近性(修改幅度小)、稀疏性(修改特征少)和可行性(时间序列时序一致)，通过对设备运行历史进行最小化修改来生成可解释的干预方案。

Result: 在C-MAPSS、N-CMAPSS以及真实Scania Component_X数据集上验证了方法的有效性，展示了其生成可操作、可解释干预措施的能力。

Conclusion: SurvCF(t)有效弥合了生存预测与处方性维护之间的鸿沟，使得AI能够为维护策略提供可解释且面向决策的支持。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance relies on accurate Remaining Useful Life estimation, often formulated using survival analysis over multivariate time-series data. While modern deep survival models achieve strong predictive performance, their black-box nature limits their use in safety-critical settings where actionable insight is required. In this work, we introduce \textit{SurvCF(t)}, the first framework for generating counterfactual explanations for survival models operating on time-series data. \textit{SurvCF(t)} identifies minimal, plausible, and temporally consistent changes to an asset's operational history that increase its predicted life time, framing explanation as a constrained optimization problem combining validity, proximity, sparsity, and plausibility. We evaluate the method on multiple benchmarks, including C-MAPSS, N-CMAPSS, and a real-world case study of the Scania Component\_X dataset, demonstrating its ability to produce actionable and interpretable interventions. Our results show that \textit{SurvCF(t)} bridges the gap between survival prediction and prescriptive maintenance, enabling explainable and decision-oriented AI for maintenance strategies.

</details>


### [89] [TurboVec: A Case Study in Cost-Efficient Private Retrieval for Enterprise RAG via Codebook-Oblivious Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16973)
*Navnit Shukla,Kamal Pandey,Omsankar Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TurboVec，一种基于TurboQuant（无需训练的代码本无关标量量化器）的开源向量索引。相比FAISS乘积量化（PQ），TurboQuant 4-bit在相同内存下Recall@5提升8.5-8.9个百分点；相比HNSW，内存减少4-8倍。通过内核级白名单过滤实现租户隔离，召回率0.86-0.93（对比后过滤基线0.09-0.19）。代码本无关设计将成员推理攻击准确率降至接近随机水平（50.0% vs PQ的57.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中的向量检索层存在两个未充分探索的挑战：（1）训练型码本量化器在多租户部署中可能泄露语料库统计信息；（2）后过滤（post-hoc filtering）实现租户隔离会降低选择性查询的召回率。

Method: 提出TurboVec，核心是TurboQuant——一种代码本无关的标量量化器，无需依赖语料库的训练。采用内核级白名单过滤（kernel-level allowlist filtering）替代传统的后过滤机制来实现租户隔离。

Result: 在DBpedia OpenAI嵌入基准（d=1536, 100K-999K向量）上，TurboQuant 4-bit相比FAISS PQ在相同内存下Recall@5提升8.5-8.9个百分点。相比HNSW（R@5=0.991）和IVF-PQ（R@5=0.840），TurboQuant内存减少4-8倍。在Snowpark Container Services上，100K向量中位查询延迟11ms（vs 707ms暴力扫描）。内核级过滤在10-1000租户下Recall@10为0.86-0.93（vs 0.09-0.19）。成员推理准确率50.0%（vs PQ的57.3%）。

Conclusion: TurboVec在无需训练的前提下实现了比IVF-PQ更高的召回率，同时大幅减少内存占用；内核级过滤有效解决了多租户隔离中的召回率退化问题；代码本无关设计提供了更强的隐私保护。局限性包括单一数据集评估、与未压缩HNSW的比较、以及仅在合成数据上进行隐私评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly power enterprise LLM applications, yet the vector retrieval layer introduces two underexplored challenges: (1) trained codebook quantizers may expose corpus statistics during index construction, creating a leakage channel in multi-tenant deployments, and (2) post-hoc filtering for tenant isolation degrades recall on selective queries. We study TurboVec, an open-source vector index built on TurboQuant - a codebook-oblivious scalar quantizer requiring no corpus-dependent training. On the DBpedia OpenAI embeddings benchmark (d=1536, 100K-999K vectors), TurboQuant 4-bit outperforms trained FAISS Product Quantization at the same memory budget by 8.5-8.9 percentage points in Recall@5 across all scales. Compared to HNSW (R@5=0.991) and IVF-PQ (R@5=0.840), TurboQuant occupies a distinct design point: higher recall than IVF-PQ without training, at 4-8x less memory than HNSW. Deployed on Snowpark Container Services, TurboVec achieves 11ms median query latency at 100K vectors versus 707ms for warehouse brute-force scan. Kernel-level allowlist filtering maintains 0.86-0.93 Recall@10 across 10-1000 tenant workloads versus 0.09-0.19 for post-filter baselines. Codebook-oblivious design reduces membership inference accuracy to near-random (50.0%) versus 57.3% for PQ codebooks. Limitations include single dataset evaluation, uncompressed HNSW comparison, and privacy evaluation on synthetic data only.

</details>


### [90] [Solver-Hard Is Not Model-Hard: A Hardness-Controlled Diagnostic for LLM Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17047)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地揭示了LLM约束求解器在随机SAT相变区域附近评估时存在的混淆问题，通过精心设计的对照实验（扩展器Tseitin vs. 阶梯Tseitin公式、鸽巢锚点等），发现尽管求解器难度差距可达51倍，但LLM在密度匹配下的准确率差距仅为-32到+20个点，且正确性与冲突代理呈错误正相关（r=+0.15），证明保持重标注会使准确率骤降93点，表明模型对表面特征敏感而非真正推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM约束求解器时通常随机SAT相变区域附近进行，导致公式密度与求解器硬度相互混淆，无法区分模型是真正具备推理能力还是仅仅利用了表面统计特征。作者旨在通过解耦密度与硬度，系统测试LLM的实例级迁移能力。

Method: 在保持子句密度和最大子句宽度匹配的条件下，对比证明困难的扩展器Tseitin公式与证明容易的阶梯Tseitin公式、鸽巢锚点公式，以及密度不匹配的对照组。使用Glucose求解器的平均冲突次数作为代理指标，测试三个LLM（每个243实例），并进行证明保持重标注实验和预注册的token消耗分析。

Result: 求解器难度差距达51倍，但LLM准确率差距仅-32到+20点，合并差距+1.7点(p=0.74)；正确性与冲突呈错误正相关(r=+0.15)；证明保持重标注使一个模型准确率下降93点；推理模型在16k上下文中对证明容易的匹配公式花费更多token并在求解器最容易的UNSAT家族上耗尽预算。

Conclusion: LLM在约束推理任务中表现出与求解器硬度解耦的准确率模式，其表现高度依赖于公式表面特征而非底层证明复杂度，表明当前模型尚未真正掌握逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: LLM constraint reasoners are often evaluated near the random-SAT phase transition, confounding density and solver hardness. We test instance-level transfer while near-matching clause density. At aligned size bins, with near-matched density and matched maximum clause width, we compare proof-hard expander-Tseitin and proof-easy ladder-Tseitin formulas, pigeonhole anchors, and density-mismatched controls. Theory separates their resolution hardness; a solver-specific Glucose mean-conflict proxy differs by up to $51\times$, and five other solvers preserve the direction. Across three included models (243 instances each; a fourth is excluded for abstention), the near-matched-density accuracy gaps range from $-32$ to $+20$ points, with a pooled gap of $+1.7$ points ($p=0.74$) and a wrong-signed correctness-versus-conflict association ($r=+0.15$). A proof-preserving relabeling lowers accuracy in all five clusters for one model (mean $-93$ points) but not another, exposing model-surface sensitivity. In a preregistered extension, provider-reported completion-token spend does not consistently increase with the proxy after accounting for formula length and censoring. At 16k, the reasoning model spends more on proof-easy matched formulas and exhausts its budget on the solver-easiest UNSAT family; the 32k C1 gap is absent. These scoped dissociations concern verdict accuracy and observed token spend, not certificate solving, exact proof length, or allocation efficiency.

</details>


### [91] [Distilled Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17247)
*Chen Wang,Zhaochun Li,Jionghao Bai,Yining Zhang,Hexuan Deng,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出蒸馏强化学习（Distilled RL），将教师监督整合到RL目标中，通过反向重要性采样裁剪、负样本重置和序列级几何归一化三个组件，在提供细粒度指导的同时选择性迁移新知识，避免了无条件模仿，在族内和跨族蒸馏场景中均显著优于标准RL和同策略蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法存在两类范式各自的问题：RL依赖粗粒度结果监督，导致信用分配困难且难以获取新知识；OPD通过KL散度无条件匹配教师logits，面临相似教师无新知识、差异大的教师指导无效的困境，限制了OPD主要在族内蒸馏中使用。

Method: 提出了Distilled RL框架，将教师监督整合到RL目标中以提供细粒度指导，包含三个关键组件：1）反向重要性采样与裁剪（reverse importance sampling with clipping）；2）负样本重置（negative sample reset）；3）序列级几何归一化（sequence-level geometric normalization）。该方法能够选择性迁移新知识，避免无条件模仿。

Result: 通过简洁可解释的案例研究证明了Distilled RL能够有效将教师模型中原本不可用的知识迁移至学生模型。在族内和跨族蒸馏设置的广泛实验中，Distilled RL在pass@1和pass@k指标上均显著优于标准RL和OPD。

Conclusion: Distilled RL通过整合教师监督到RL目标中，成功克服了RL和OPD两种范式的固有缺陷，既提供了细粒度的学习信号，又能选择性迁移知识，是一种更有效的LLM后训练方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training is essential for improving reasoning, adaptation, and alignment. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, RL relies on coarse-grained outcome supervision, resulting in difficult credit assignment and limited capability to acquire new knowledge. OPD, meanwhile, unconditionally matches teacher logits through KL divergence, which creates a dilemma: similar teachers provide little new knowledge, while substantially different teachers often yield ineffective guidance, largely restricting OPD to within-family distillation. We propose Distilled Reinforcement Learning (Distilled RL), which integrates teacher supervision into the RL objective to provide fine-grained guidance, selectively transfer new knowledge and avoid unconditional imitation. Distilled RL contains three components: reverse importance sampling with clipping, negative sample reset, and sequence-level geometric normalization. Through a concise and interpretable case study, we demonstrate that Distilled RL can effectively transfer previously unavailable knowledge from a teacher model to a student model. Extensive experiments across both within-family and cross-family distillation settings show that Distilled RL substantially outperforms standard RL and OPD in terms of both pass@1 and pass@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/Distilled-RL.

</details>


### [92] [Rationalizing Boltzmann Rationality: An Axiomatic Characterization of Entropy-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17316)
*Silviu Pitis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过区分环境随机性（机会）和策略随机性（选择），证明在马尔可夫决策过程中施加无关选项独立性（IIA）和单调性公理，可唯一导出玻尔兹曼策略、熵正则化表示和软贝尔曼方程，从而解决了软贝尔曼方程中熵奖励与MDP独立性公理之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中softmax策略是随机选择的默认模型，但现有文献缺乏从第一性原理出发的唯一推导。软贝尔曼方程中的熵奖励违反了支撑MDP奖励结构的独立性公理，这一基本张力尚未得到解决。

Method: 将VNM独立性公理限制在环境彩票（机会）上，对策略和价值函数施加IIA和单调性公理，从公理化角度推导出玻尔兹曼策略、熵正则化表示和软贝尔曼方程的唯一形式。

Result: 软贝尔曼方程与硬贝尔曼方程之间的选择简化为一个设计决策：智能体是否珍视自身的选择能力。建立了回报单调性和广义折扣下的收敛性等RL性质，综合了经济学和信息理论中导向相同结构的独立研究路线。

Conclusion: 对IIA在智能体设计中何时适用提供了规范性评估，从公理基础解决了softmax策略在RL中的合理性问题。

Abstract: The softmax policy $π(a \mid s) \propto \exp(βQ(s,a))$ is the default model of stochastic choice in reinforcement learning (RL). Various justifications based on robustness, exploration, and optimization have been offered in the RL literature, but none uniquely derives the softmax form from first principles. This leaves a basic tension unresolved: the entropy bonus in the soft Bellman equation violates the Independence axiom that underwrites the Markov decision process (MDP) reward structure. We dissolve this tension by distinguishing two kinds of randomness: chance and choice. By restricting von Neumann-Morgenstern (VNM) Independence to environmental lotteries over base prospects, we show that imposing independence of irrelevant alternatives (IIA) and monotonicity on the policy and value functions at choice nodes uniquely determines the Boltzmann policy, the entropy-regularized representation, and the soft Bellman equation. The choice between the soft and hard Bellman equations thus reduces to a design decision: whether the agent values its own ability to choose. We develop RL-specific consequences, including return monotonicity and convergence under generalized discounting, and synthesize the independent lines from economics and information theory that arrive at the same structure, offering a normative assessment of when IIA is appropriate for agent design.

</details>


### [93] [CoEvoP&R: Co-Evolving Placement Objectives with Routing Feedback via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17398)
*Ruogu Chen,Weihua Xiao,Ramesh Karri,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoEvoP&R 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，能够自动演化分析性布局的可微目标函数，通过结合布局、时序代理和布线工具的反馈，迭代生成更好的目标函数。在多个基准测试上，相比原生 DREAMPlace，后布线长度减少 5.4%-16.9%，拥塞减少 23.2%-36.7%，最差负松弛提升 0.70 ns，总负松弛减少 912 ns。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析性布局器依赖的中间代理指标（如 HPWL 和单元密度惩罚）与最终布线及时序质量之间存在不对齐问题。手工设计的项需要专家反复调参，而黑盒代理方法难以解释、调试和部署。

Method: CoEvoP&R 利用大语言模型（LLM）来自动演化分析性布局的目标函数。每轮迭代中，prompt 包含受限目标接口、基线上下文、历史候选目标以及来自布局、时序代理和布线工具的反馈。LLM 提出可读的可微目标函数，在 DREAMPlace 中嵌入并验证，通过时序代理和实际路由器评估，并将结果与反馈存储，用于指导后续迭代。

Result: 在 8 个 ChiP-Bench Nangate45 设计上，后布线长度减少 16.9%，拥塞减少 36.7%，最差负松弛提升 0.70 ns，总负松弛减少 912 ns。在 8 个 ICCAD 2015 Superblue 设计上，后布线长度减少 5.4%，拥塞减少 23.2%。

Conclusion: CoEvoP&R 通过 LLM 自动演化目标函数，有效缩小了布局代理指标与布线及时序质量之间的差距，在多项指标上显著优于原生 DREAMPlace，且生成的函数可读、可解释，便于在分析性布局流程中部署。

Abstract: Analytical placers rely on differentiable objective functions to guide placement, typically combining intermediate surrogate metrics such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) and cell-density penalties. However, these placement-stage surrogates remain misaligned with downstream routed and timing quality. Prior work reduces this gap with human-designed terms or learned black-box surrogates, but the former requires expert retuning and the latter is difficult to explain, debug, or deploy in analytical placement flows. CoEvoP&R addresses these limitations with a large language model (LLM)-based framework that automatically evolves analytical placement objectives. At each generation, the prompt combines the restricted objective interface, baseline context, and archived prior candidates with routing-related feedback from placement, timing proxy, and routing tools. The LLM proposes readable differentiable objectives, which are embedded and validated in DREAMPlace, evaluated through a timing proxy and an actual router, and stored with their feedback to guide later generations. Across eight ChiP-Bench Nangate45 designs and three seeds, CoEvoP&R reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 16.9% and 36.7%, with gains of 0.70 ns in worst negative slack and a 912 ns reduction in total negative slack magnitude over native DREAMPlace. Across eight ICCAD 2015 Superblue designs, it reduces post-route routed wirelength and congestion by 5.4% and 23.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/FCHXWH823/CoEvoP-R.git.

</details>


### [94] [CORAL: Learning Amyloid Fibril Ligand Docking with Cooperative Binding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17412)
*Yasheng Sun,Bohan Li,Youqi Tao,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CORAL框架，利用强化学习训练生成式对接模型，专门针对淀粉样纤维的交叉β沟槽几何结构进行配体对接，通过引入配体间协同堆叠能量作为奖励，解决了现有对接方法无法处理淀粉样纤维独特结合模式的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经退行性疾病中蛋白质异常聚集形成淀粉样纤维，小分子配体选择性结合这些纤维具有诊断、成像和治疗潜力。然而，淀粉样纤维-配体共晶结构极其稀缺，无法进行监督训练；且淀粉样纤维的配体结合模式（配体插入纵向交叉β沟槽并沿纤维轴协同堆叠）与球状蛋白完全不同，现有对接模型无法捕捉这种几何特性。

Method: 提出CORAL（COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking）强化学习框架，训练生成式对接模型产生适配交叉β沟槽几何的配体姿态分布。奖励函数同时包含蛋白质-配体对接亲和力和配体间协同堆叠能量，直接捕获淀粉样纤维的独特结合几何。此外构建了一个由领域专家验证的模型生成姿态组成的淀粉样纤维-配体复合物评估集。

Result: 在实验解析结构和该评估集上的实验表明，CORAL在姿态质量和结合亲和力相关性上均优于现有对接基线方法。

Conclusion: CORAL通过强化学习有效解决了淀粉样纤维这一特殊靶标的配体对接难题，为神经退行性疾病相关的小分子药物设计提供了新工具。

Abstract: A hallmark of neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's and Parkinson's is the aberrant aggregation of proteins into amyloid fibrils, and small molecules that selectively bind to these fibrils hold promise as diagnostics, imaging probes, and therapeutics. Predicting how such ligands bind to fibril targets, however, presents two fundamental challenges. First, resolved co-crystal structures of amyloid-ligand complexes are exceptionally scarce; even with recent advances in cryo-EM only a handful have been structurally characterized, making supervised training of docking models impractical for this target class. Second, amyloid fibrils present a binding mode fundamentally different from globular proteins: ligands intercalate into longitudinal cross-$β$ grooves and stack cooperatively along the fibril axis, a geometry that existing docking models are not designed to capture. To address these challenges, we present CORAL (COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking), a reinforcement learning framework that trains a generative docking model to produce ligand pose distributions tailored to the cross-$β$ groove geometry. Our reward explicitly incorporates cooperative ligand-ligand stacking energy alongside protein-ligand docking affinity, directly capturing the distinctive binding geometry of amyloid fibrils. We further introduce a curated evaluation set of amyloid-ligand complexes constructed from model-generated poses validated by domain experts. Experiments on both experimentally resolved structures and this evaluation set demonstrate improved pose quality and binding affinity correlation over existing docking baselines.

</details>


### [95] [Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419)
*Ayoub Ghriss,Sourav Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KATA框架，通过自对偶齐次锥推导特征映射，将注意力召回建模为球面填充问题。秩一PSD特征在容量-干扰间取得最优折衷，支持无参数凸输出门。在131k token上实现约11倍FlashAttention-2吞吐量，长程MQAR保留0.985准确率，且仅需约1/4的KV缓存条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力在理论上具有常数时间循环推理的优势，但在关联召回任务上性能严重退化。本文旨在解决线性注意力在关联召回中的固有缺陷，从第一性原理出发设计能够保证非负注意力权重的核化特征映射。

Method: 将注意力召回形式化为球面填充问题，通过自对偶齐次锥认证非负注意力权重，推导出核化线性注意力激活（KATA）框架。提出秩一PSD特征，利用Welch干扰界刻画关联容量。实现两种Triton内核：类flash-attention前向传播（~1.6倍FlashAttention-2吞吐量）和精确O(T)分块状态形式（131k token上~11倍FlashAttention-2吞吐量）。采用一阶特征的关联扫描将块间递归深度降至O(log(T/C))。

Result: KATA在长程MQAR和重复键覆盖任务上优于Gated DeltaNet；在16倍分布外长度上保留0.985 MQAR准确率，接近softmax注意力且仅用约1/4 KV缓存条目。340M参数LLM实验揭示了特征相关的流畅度权衡，并阐明了位置编码、delta规则和衰减门与特征几何的交互作用。

Conclusion: KATA从理论上统一了线性注意力的容量-干扰折衷分析，提供了可证明的非负注意力权重保证和高效实现。在长序列场景下，KATA在吞吐量和召回性能上均显著优于现有线性注意力方法，为高效Transformer的线性化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Linear attention promises constant-time recurrent inference but degrades sharply on associative recall. We formulate attention recall as a spherical-packing problem and introduce Kernelized Linear Attention Activations (KATA), a framework whose feature maps are derived from first principles by certifying nonnegative attention weights through a self-dual homogeneous cone. Building on this observation, we show that rank-one positive semi-definite (PSD) features offer a favorable capacity--interference tradeoff. KATA recovers a parameter-free convex output gate and characterizes associative capacity through the Welch interference floor. For tolerances above this floor, KATA enlarges the state without adding parameters and admits spherical codes with exponentially many keys in the projection dimension. We implement KATA as fused Triton kernels at two operating points: a flash-attention-style forward up to ${\sim}1.6\times$ FlashAttention-2 throughput, and an exact $O(T)$ chunked-state form that reaches ${\sim}11\times$ FlashAttention-2 forward throughput at $131$k tokens. An associative scan of the first-order feature lowers the inter-chunk recurrence depth to $O(\log(T/C))$ for chunk size $C$ and averages ${\sim}2.4\times$ the throughput of a matched sequential linear-attention baseline. On long-range MQAR and repeated-key overwrite, several KATA variants outperform Gated DeltaNet, with parameter counts and state sizes reported alongside accuracy. Induction preserves near-perfect recall, while kernel benchmarks show that the maps can be implemented efficiently. KATA retains $0.985$ MQAR at a $16\times$ out-of-distribution length, approaching the softmax with roughly one quarter of the KV-cache entries. Experiments on 340M-parameter LLMs reveal a feature-dependent fluency trade-off and clarify how positional embeddings, delta rules, and decay gates interact with feature geometry.

</details>


### [96] [FailureAtlas: A Taxonomy of Failure Modes in Multi-Provider LLM Serving Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17525)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双轴分类法（\fa{}），用于分类多提供商LLM网关中的故障模式，按起源层（网络/传输、流/协议、状态/会话、模型行为、治理/成本）和可检测性（显性 vs. 隐性）进行划分。通过五个验证过的故障案例（含三个可复现脚本）发现，最严重的运维故障是隐性的——返回HTTP 200、通过健康检查但破坏应用状态，需语义级可观测性才能检测。其中两个隐性故障（并发竞态导致历史丢失、流式索引冲突破坏工具调用载荷）是在评估过程中首次发现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多提供商LLM网关已成为生产关键基础设施，但其特定的故障模式从未被系统化文档化，仅分散在问题追踪和故障报告中，缺乏统一的分类框架。

Method: 提出双轴分类法（\fa{}）：按故障起源层（5类）和可检测性（显性/隐性）分类。从公开bug报告和第一手压力测试中收集并验证了5个故障案例，每个案例附带机制性根因分析，其中3个提供独立复现脚本。

Result: 发现最严重的运维故障是隐性故障：返回HTTP 200、通过所有标准健康检查，但破坏应用状态，需语义级可观测性才能检测。两个首次发现的隐性故障：并发竞态导致对话历史丢失，流式索引冲突破坏工具调用载荷。

Conclusion: 当前LLM网关的监控手段（健康检查、HTTP状态码）不足以检测最危险的隐性故障，需要引入语义级别的可观测性工具来保障生产系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-provider LLM gateways reverse proxies that route, load-balance, and rate-limit requests across foundation-model APIs have become critical production infrastructure. Yet the failure modes specific to this architectural layer remain undocumented, scattered across issue trackers and post-mortems with no unifying framework. We introduce \fa{}, a two-axis taxonomy that classifies failures by their \emph{origin layer} (Network/Transport, Streaming/Protocol, State/Session, Model~Behavior, Governance/Cost) and their \emph{detectability} (Loud vs.\ Silent). We populate this taxonomy with five verified catalog entries sourced from public bug reports and first-hand stress testing, each accompanied by a mechanistic root-cause analysis. Three entries include standalone reproduction scripts. Our principal finding is that the most operationally severe failures are \emph{silent}: they return HTTP~200, pass every standard health check, and corrupt application state in ways that require semantic-level observability to detect. Two such silent failures a concurrency race condition causing history loss and a streaming index collision corrupting tool-call payloads were discovered first-hand during \cb{} evaluation campaigns.

</details>


### [97] [Program Synthesis for Simulation-Based Inference: Joint Model Selection and Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17540)
*Siddharth Mishra-Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合大型语言模型（LLM）程序合成与神经模拟推断的框架，用于自动进行模型选择与参数估计。LLM根据自然语言描述生成候选模拟程序，通过反馈驱动变异迭代优化，并利用神经密度估计进行评估，从而实现对模型池而非固定模型参数的推断。在确定性动力学、随机流行病模型和引力透镜暗物质子结构推断基准上，该方法能从开放式提示中识别出合理的模型族，准确性取决于数据信息量和模型可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经模拟推断方法要求用户预先指定一个编码固定模型结构的模拟器，这限制了模型探索的灵活性。本文旨在解决如何同时进行模型选择与参数估计的问题，使得研究者无需手动指定模型结构，即可从数据中自动发现合理的模型族及其参数。

Method: 该方法结合LLM程序合成与神经模拟推断。LLM根据自然语言描述和初始数据生成候选模拟器程序，通过反馈驱动的变异（如基于模拟-推断误差的迭代修正）对程序进行改进，并利用神经密度估计评估各模型的后验概率，从而实现模型池内的联合推断。

Result: 在确定性动力学、随机流行病模型和引力透镜暗物质子结构推断三个基准上，该方法能从开放式自然语言提示中自动识别出合理的模型族，且模型选择的准确性随数据信息量的增加和模型可辨识性的提高而提升。

Conclusion: 本文展示了LLM与神经模拟推断结合的有效性，证明该方法能够在无需人工指定模型结构的情况下实现自动化的模型发现与参数推断，为复杂系统的科学建模提供了一种灵活、数据驱动的框架。

Abstract: Neural simulation-based inference enables parameter estimation for complex models, but typically requires the user to specify a simulator encoding a fixed model structure. We present a framework for joint model selection and parameter estimation that combines large language models for program synthesis with neural simulation-based inference. Given a natural language description of the system and data under investigation, an LLM proposes candidate simulator programs which are iteratively refined via feedback-driven mutation and evaluated using neural density estimation. The approach enables simulation-based inference over a pool of models, not just parameters within a fixed model. On benchmarks spanning deterministic dynamics, stochastic epidemic models, and dark matter substructure inference from gravitational-lensing images, the method identifies plausible model families from open-ended prompts, with accuracy that reflects the information content of the data and identifiability of candidate models.

</details>


### [98] [CoCurve: Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature for Training-Free Structured LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17568)
*Zhiren Gong,Zihao Zeng,Zijie Wang,Tiantong Wang,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoCurve提出了一种无需微调的跨模块结构化剪枝方法，通过Fisher矩阵的块对角加稀疏非对角结构联合建模注意力头与FFN通道组之间的剪枝交互，将剪枝问题转化为单次二次规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练剪枝方法将各计算单元独立评分，忽略了Transformer中残差流耦合导致的单元间依赖关系，导致两个单独重要但联合移除时损失巨大的单元被同时剪掉。

Method: 利用冻结模型与掩码副本之间token级KL散度的二阶泰勒展开，构造一个Fisher矩阵：对角线为经典节点显著性，非对角线为共剪枝曲率边（联合移除两个单元的额外损伤）。在单次消融可加性近似下，该矩阵退化为单单元消融特征的Gram积，从而通过M次前向传播（无成对扫描或梯度）恢复完整的M×M交互矩阵。剪枝转化为一个带预算的二次规划问题，一次性求解。

Result: 在共享注意力-FFN预算下，无需标签、微调或恢复步骤，一次性完成剪枝。

Conclusion: CoCurve通过建模跨模块剪枝交互，在无需微调的前提下实现了更准确的结构化剪枝决策，弥补了独立评分方法的理论缺陷。

Abstract: Structured pruning compresses large language models (LLMs) by removing whole computational units, such as attention heads and feed-forward (FFN) channel groups. Most training-free methods, however, rank these units independently, implicitly treating the loss from pruning a set as the sum of its individual losses. This view fails for Transformers, whose sublayers are coupled through a shared residual stream. Two individually weak units can thus be jointly indispensable, yet independent scoring is blind to such dependence and removes them together. We introduce CoCurve (Cross-Module Co-Pruning Curvature), a calibration-only, fine-tuning-free method that prunes attention and FFN units jointly. A second-order Taylor expansion of the token-level KL between the frozen model and its masked copy yields a single Fisher matrix whose diagonal is classical node saliency and whose off-diagonal entries are co-pruning curvature edges: the extra damage of removing two units together. Under a single-ablation additivity approximation this matrix reduces to a Gram product of single-unit ablation features, so the full M x M interaction is recovered from M forward passes, with no pairwise sweeps or gradients. Pruning then reduces to one budgeted quadratic program, solved in a single shot under a shared attention--FFN budget, with no labels, fine-tuning, or recovery.

</details>


### [99] [Trustworthy Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction via Reliability-Aware Multi-Engine Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17601)
*Yongchan Hong,Defu Cao,Wenjin Liu,Thomas Ku,Jordy Homing Lam,Emily Nguyen,Willie Neiswanger,Vsevolod Katritch,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RELIABLE-BA 是一个基于证据融合的多引擎蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测框架，通过 Normal-Inverse-Gamma 分布建模每个引擎、从分子上下文学习可靠性缩放认知不确定性、以及封闭形式聚合来融合专家，在保持竞争性点预测的同时大幅改善不确定性校准，并能筛选高置信度预测将误差降低 25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子对接引擎常产生分歧且缺乏可信度指示，共识评分和集成方法虽提升平均精度但缺乏可解释的置信度度量或不确定性分解，忽略了每个蛋白质-配体对的化学上下文。

Method: 三步流程：(1) 通过 Normal-Inverse-Gamma 分布将每个引擎建模为证据专家；(2) 从分子上下文学习可靠性以缩放认知不确定性，同时保留每个专家的预测均值；(3) 通过封闭形式聚合融合专家，同时捕捉个体不确定性和引擎间分歧。

Result: 在 PDBBind 和 BDB2020+ 基准上取得竞争性点预测并显著改善不确定性校准；在 SARS-CoV-2 Mpro 和 5HT2A 受体上验证了临床相关药物靶点的适用性；高置信度筛选可降低预测误差达 25%。

Conclusion: RELIABLE-BA 是首个结合证据融合与上下文相关可靠性的多引擎结合亲和力预测框架，为可信赖的 AI 驱动药物发现提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: Accurate protein-ligand binding affinity prediction is central to computational drug discovery, yet modern docking engines frequently disagree without indicating which prediction to trust. Consensus scoring and ensemble methods improve mean accuracy but treat all predictions identically without interpretable confidence measures or uncertainty decomposition, ignoring the chemical context of each protein-ligand pair. To address this limitation, we introduce RELIABLE-BA (RELIABiLity-aware Evidential fusion for Binding Affinity), an evidential framework for multi-engine binding affinity prediction. Our model comprises three steps: (1) modeling each engine as an evidential expert via Normal-Inverse-Gamma distributions, (2) scaling epistemic uncertainty through learned reliability from molecular context while preserving each expert's predictive mean, and (3) fusing experts through closed-form aggregation that captures both individual uncertainty and inter-engine disagreement. Experiments on the PDBBind and BDB2020+ benchmarks demonstrate competitive point prediction with substantially improved uncertainty calibration, and additional validation on the SARS-CoV-2 Mpro dataset and 5HT2A receptor demonstrates applicability to clinically relevant drug targets. Crucially, these uncertainty estimates enable reliable filtering of protein-ligand pairs, reducing prediction error by up to 25% when retaining only high-confidence pairs. To our knowledge, RELIABLE-BA is the first multi-engine binding affinity prediction framework to combine evidential fusion with context-dependent reliability, offering a principled path toward trustworthy AI-guided drug discovery. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yongchand/RELIABLE-BA.

</details>


### [100] [TypiCore: A Hybrid Active Query Strategy for Class-Incremental Learning on Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17632)
*Gabor Szucs,Samuel Jacsev,Marcell Nemeth,Davide Dalle Pezze,Gian Antonio Susto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多变量时间序列的主动类增量学习（ACIL），提出了一种名为TypiCore的混合查询策略，该策略在主动学习周期中交替进行典型性驱动和多样性驱动的样本选择，在有限标注预算下构建兼具代表性和多样性的记忆缓冲区，在TSCIL基准上显著优于所有基线方法，并在多个数据集上以极少的标注量达到或超越全监督持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法面临一个关键限制：真实世界数据流很少完全标注，标注成本是主要的实际约束。因此需要研究如何在固定标注预算下，让模型在顺序学习新类的同时选择性查询标签。

Method: 提出TypiCore混合查询策略，在主动学习周期中交替使用基于典型性（typicality-based）和基于多样性（diversity-based）的样本选择方法，构建兼具代表性和多样性的记忆缓冲区。

Result: 在TSCIL基准上，TypiCore取得统计显著的性能提升，超越所有基线方法，并在多个数据集上以极少的标注量达到或超越全监督持续学习性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性方法和分布感知方法在有限标注预算下表现受限，而TypiCore通过混合策略有效解决了这一问题，为多变量时间序列的主动类增量学习提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Time series data play a pivotal role across numerous domains, including healthcare and manufacturing. In real-world environments, models must cope with distribution shifts over time, a challenge commonly addressed through Continual Learning (CL) techniques. However, existing CL methods face a critical limitation: real-world data streams are rarely fully labeled, making annotation cost a major practical constraint. This paper investigates Active Class-Incremental Learning (ACIL) for multivariate time series, where a model must sequentially learn new classes while selectively querying labels under a fixed annotation budget. We present a systematic evaluation of a wide range of query strategies combined with multiple rehearsal-based approaches, assessing their impact on plasticity, stability, and label efficiency across four benchmark datasets. Our analysis reveals the limitations of uncertainty-based and distribution-aware methods in achieving strong performance under constrained labeling budgets. To address these shortcomings, we propose TypiCore, a novel hybrid query strategy that alternates between typicality-based and diversity-based sample selection across active learning cycles, enabling the construction of memory buffers that are both representative and diverse. Evaluated on the TSCIL benchmark, TypiCore delivers statistically significant improvements over all baselines and matches or surpasses fully supervised continual learning performance on multiple datasets while requiring a fraction of the available labels.

</details>


### [101] [MXSens: Sensitivity-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17733)
*Simla Burcu Harma,Danila Mishin,Zhengyuan Su,Ayan Chakraborty,Elizaveta Kostenok,Dongho Ha,Babak Falsafi,Martin Jaggi,Yunho Oh,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MXSens，一种无需训练、基于敏感度的混合精度量化方法，利用MXINT格式的块结构，根据列和层的敏感度分配4/6/8位尾数位宽，在W4A4KV4设置下显著提升LLM量化精度，例如LLaMA-2-70B困惑度达3.77，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4-bit量化因异常值导致精度严重下降；先前方法（数据旋转或混合精度整数量化）依赖软件管理缩放和频繁反量化，开销大；MXINT格式虽可在硬件中编码缩放因子，但与旋转方法不兼容。因此需要一种无需训练、兼容MXINT硬件的高效量化方案。

Method: MXSens首先分析发现异常值严重程度不一（从罕见极值到频繁轻微偏差），且量化敏感度在层和列上分布不均。据此提出基于列和层敏感度的混合尾数位宽分配策略（4/6/8位），利用MXINT天然的块结构进行分组量化，无需额外训练或旋转。

Result: 在W4A4KV4设置下，LLaMA-2-70B困惑度达3.77，LLaMA-3-8B达7.63（WikiText-2），显著优于现有基线方法。在多种模型和任务上均超过当前最先进的量化方法。

Conclusion: MXSens在LLM量化中建立了精度与资源效率之间的新平衡，证明了基于敏感度的混合精度策略结合MXINT硬件格式的有效性。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization enables efficient LLM inference, but suffers from significant accuracy degradation due to outliers. Prior work addresses this problem via data rotation or mixed-precision integer quantization, but often relies on software-managed scaling and frequent dequantization, incurring substantial overhead. Microscaling formats, such as MXINT, eliminate these inefficiencies by encoding scales in hardware, yet remain incompatible with rotation-based methods. Our analysis reveals that outliers vary in severity, from rare extremes to frequent mild deviations, and that quantization sensitivity is unevenly distributed across layers and columns. These insights motivate a fine-grained, sensitivity-guided approach. We introduce MXSens, a training-free method that assigns mixed mantissa bitwidths (4/6/8) based on column- and layer-wise sensitivity, naturally leveraging the block-wise structure of MXINT. MXSens outperforms state-of-the-art quantization methods across a range of models and tasks. Under the W4A4KV4 setting, MXSens achieves perplexities of 3.77 and 7.63 on LLaMA-2-70B and LLaMA-3-8B, respectively, substantially improving over existing baselines on WikiText-2. Our work establishes a new balance between accuracy and resource efficiency for LLM quantization.

</details>


### [102] [FIFA World Cup 2026 as a Contamination-Free Benchmark for LLM Forecasting Agents: Four Models, a Bookmaker, and 104 Matches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17765)
*Jiacheng Ding,Cong Guo,Jason Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了WC2026-Agents基准，用于评估LLM作为自主预测智能体对2026年世界杯104场比赛的预测能力。四个前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.8、ChatGPT GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Grok Expert Mode）执行相同的搜索-行动-反思循环，与博彩市场赔率对比。结果显示：四个模型在92%的比赛中选择相同的最优选项，但无一超越市场的Brier分数；然而它们在决策质量上差异显著（投资回报率从-18%到+10%不等），衡量了校准度、决策质量和自我认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估缺乏对模型作为自主预测智能体在真实、未来事件上的系统评估，且存在数据污染问题。需要一个无污染、有经济基线、能衡量校准度和决策质量的基准框架。

Method: 选取2026年FIFA世界杯104场比赛，四个前沿模型执行统一搜索-行动-反思循环：使用网络工具收集证据，提交1X2（主胜/平/客胜）分布和100美元虚拟投注，赛后仅根据最终比分进行反思。同时收集每场比赛赛前博彩市场1X2赔率作为经济基线。共产生416个预测和414次反思。

Result: 1) 四个模型在92%的比赛中选择相同的最优选项；2) 没有一个模型能超越市场的Brier分数；3) 简单平注市场热门比所有四个智能体收益更高；4) 智能体在投资回报率（-18%至+10%）、引用市场的比例（12%-100%）、自我报告错误率（36%-86%）上差异显著；5) 反向市场策略对所有模型均不盈利。

Conclusion: 即使前沿模型在原始预测上趋于一致，它们在决策质量和自我认知等维度上存在显著差异。该基准为评估LLM作为自主智能体的校准度、决策质量和自我认知提供了有效框架，弥补了单纯原始准确率评估的不足。

Abstract: We introduce WC2026-Agents, a benchmark and dataset for evaluating large language models (LLMs) as autonomous forecasting agents on real, future events. For every one of the 104 matches of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, four frontier models -- Claude Opus 4.8, ChatGPT (GPT-5.5, high reasoning), Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Grok (Expert Mode) -- ran an identical search-act-reflect loop: gather evidence with a web tool, commit to a 1X2 (team-A win / draw / team-B win) distribution and a virtual 100-USD bet, and, after the match, reflect given only the final score. Because every match kicked off after the models' training cutoffs, the benchmark is contamination-free by construction. Crucially, we pair the four agents with a fifth competitor drawn from the same information environment -- the pre-match betting market -- collected as per-match 1X2 odds, giving an economically grounded baseline and letting us score not just what an agent predicts but what it does with money. The release contains 416 forecasts and 414 reflections with verbatim reasoning, ground truth (including penalty shootouts), odds, and a reproducible evaluation suite. A reference evaluation surfaces findings that raw accuracy hides: the four agents issue an identical top pick in 92% of matches and none beats the market's Brier score; indeed, a naive flat stake on the market favorite out-earns all four agents. Yet the agents diverge sharply as decision-makers: betting return-on-investment ranges from -18% to +10%, fading the market is unprofitable for all four, the share of forecasts that cite the market ranges from 12% to 100%, and self-reported error rates on wrong picks range from 36% to 86%. The benchmark thus measures calibration, decision quality, and self-knowledge -- axes on which frontier models differ even when their predictions do not. Data and code: https://github.com/graphuofm/FIFA2026LLM

</details>


### [103] [Topological Signatures of Context-Level Reliability in TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17962)
*James Hu,Mahdi Ghelichi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用之字形持续同调（zigzag persistent homology）分析TabPFN在结构化困难的表格数据任务中的内部表征拓扑，发现拓扑特征（如H₀碎片计数、H₁环路活性）与模型可靠性（绝对残差、过置信度）强相关，可作为诊断模型是否处于拓扑压力状态的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN在表格预测任务中表现优异，但其内部行为在结构困难的表格几何上仍缺乏理解，因此有必要研究其在复杂拓扑任务下的表征几何与模型可靠性之间的关系。

Method: 构建包含扭曲圆环、环面、球面、Hopf链、三叶结、Swiss卷等合成表格任务的受控基准，将TabPFN各层表示视为演化点云，应用之字形持续同调分析其拓扑特征（H₀碎片计数、H₁持续性和环路活性）。

Result: H₀碎片计数与平均绝对残差正相关；困难几何引发H₁环路活性增加和H₀碎片增加，但H₁持续性变短；这些拓扑描述符与贝叶斯误差、绝对残差和过置信度相关。

Conclusion: 之字形持续同调能够诊断TabPFN推断的上下文任务几何的可靠性，并提供了识别模型处于拓扑压力状态的上下文级视角。

Abstract: TabPFN is a transformer-based foundation model for tabular prediction that performs inference without task-specific training by conditioning on a support set and query inputs. Despite its strong empirical performance, its internal behavior on structurally difficult tabular geometries remains poorly understood. We study this behavior using zigzag persistent homology, treating TabPFN layer representations as evolving point clouds. We construct a controlled benchmark of synthetic tabular tasks with known true probabilities and varied intrinsic topology, including warped circles, tori, spheres, Hopf links, trefoil knots, and Swiss rolls. Across these tasks, we find that the topology of TabPFN's internal representation geometry is strongly associated with dataset-level reliability; for example, the zeroth homology group $H_0$ fragmentation count correlates positively with mean absolute residual across controlled tasks, and this association strengthens in a high-resolution warped circle case study at large sample size. Harder geometries induce a dual topological signature: increased $H_1$ loop activity and increased $H_0$ fragmentation, while the $H_1$ persistence becomes shorter-lived. These descriptors correlate with Bayes error, mean absolute residuals, and overconfidence. Our results suggest that zigzag persistence diagnoses the reliability of the inferred in-context task geometry and provides a context-level view of when TabPFN operates in topologically stressed regimes.

</details>


### [104] [Harness Engineering for LLM-Driven GPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17979)
*Yue Shui,Chenyu Ma,Hangfei Xu,Shengzhao Wen,Yanpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种以评估框架为中心的LLM驱动GPU内核优化系统，在NVIDIA Blackwell B200 GPU上针对FlashInfer竞赛的五个算子定义实现了1.12x至29.68x的加速比，并展示了人类专家引导对AI驱动内核优化的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以辅助GPU内核生成，但其实际有效性取决于生成的代码能否被可靠地约束、验证、性能分析和选择。现有方法缺乏系统化的框架来确保生成内核的质量和可复现性。

Method: 设计了一个分离式系统：评估框架（harness）负责编译、正确性验证、官方对齐计时和工件归档；性能分析驱动的优化控制器（controller）将性能分析器和工作负载证据转化为有边界的候选生成决策。人类专家编写技能（skills）捕获算子约束、参考实现、性能分析流程和提升规则，Codex和Claude Code代理在约束内生成候选内核。

Result: 在五个算子定义上，官方对齐的工件相比FlashInfer基准实现了平均延迟加速比分别为1.62x、18.05x、29.68x、1.12x和13.70x。Agent-Assisted内核在所有评估定义上均优于Full-Agent工件。

Conclusion: 专家提供的优化方向、高质量的参考实现和工作负载上下文对于可靠的AI驱动内核优化仍然至关重要，纯自主代理方法尚无法超越人类引导的优化效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can assist GPU kernel generation, but their practical effectiveness depends on whether generated code can be reliably constrained, validated, profiled, and selected. This paper presents a harness-centered system for LLM-driven GPU kernel optimization in the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer AI Kernel Generation Contest on NVIDIA Blackwell B200 GPUs. The system separates an evaluation harness from a profile-backed optimization controller: the harness enforces compilation, correctness, official-aligned timing, and artifact archival, while the controller turns profiler and workload evidence into bounded candidate-generation decisions. Human-authored skills capture operator constraints, references, profiling procedures, and promotion rules, while Codex and Claude Code agents generate candidate kernels inside those constraints. Across five operator definitions, the retained official-aligned artifacts achieved mean-latency speedups over supplied FlashInfer baselines of 1.62x, 18.05x, 29.68x, 1.12x, and 13.70x. The Agent-Assisted kernels outperform the Full-Agent artifacts across the evaluated definitions, indicating that expert-provided optimization directions, high-quality references, and workload context remain critical for reliable AI-driven kernel optimization.

</details>


### [105] [Sobek: Streaming Equivariant Tensor Product Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18074)
*Vladimir Chorošajev,Cédric Bény*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sobek——一种流式执行方案，通过重排径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合的顺序，将边级张量积中间结果直接消费到接收端状态中，消除了传统等变图神经网络中随图规模和算子宽度急剧增长的边级中间变量。在75项容量匹配对比中，Sobek均优于OpenEquivariance，加速比1.2×至49.7×，峰值内存降低高达99%，并能执行超出后者两个数量级的工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等变图神经网络在实现边条件张量积卷积时，会具体化边级权重、消息和伴随变量，导致张量积工作空间和内存流量随图规模和算子宽度快速增长，限制了可行工作负载并阻碍更大问题充分利用GPU。

Method: 通过重关联径向投影、球谐耦合和图聚合三个步骤，将边局部乘积直接消费到有界的接收端状态中，形成流式执行方案，保持全连接多重态混合，并扩展至前向、反向和双反向传播。基于生成CUDA后端实现为Sobek。

Result: 在两个算子家族和三个微分阶次上的75项容量匹配对比中，Sobek均更快，加速比1.2×到49.7×；峰值内存降低高达99%；能执行超出OpenEquivariance容量两个数量级的工作负载，同时保持近峰值吞吐量。

Conclusion: 边级张量积工作空间是传统执行调度的产物，而非等变卷积本身的固有需求。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks repeatedly apply edge-conditioned tensor-product convolutions over graph edges. Conventional implementations materialize edge-specific weights, messages, and adjoints, causing tensor-product workspace and memory traffic to grow rapidly with graph size and operator width. This limits feasible workloads and can prevent larger problems from fully utilizing the GPU.
  We show that these edge-sized intermediates are artifacts of the execution schedule, not requirements of the equivariant operator. By reassociating radial projection, spherical-harmonic coupling, and graph aggregation, edge-local products can be consumed directly into bounded receiver-side state. The resulting streaming formulation preserves fully connected multiplicity mixing and extends through forward, backward, and double backward.
  We implement this formulation in Sobek, a generated-CUDA backend, and evaluate it across edge-scaling regimes and varied feature structures. Across two operator families and all three differentiation orders, Sobek is faster in all 75 capacity-matched comparisons, with speedups ranging from $1.2\times$ to $49.7\times$, and reduces peak allocated memory by up to 99\%. It also executes workloads up to two orders of magnitude beyond OpenEquivariance's capacity while retaining near-peak throughput. These results show that edge-scaled tensor-product workspace is a property of the conventional schedule, not of equivariant convolution itself.

</details>


### [106] [SelectInfer: Selective Neuron Loading and Computation for On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18081)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Eric Schniedermeyer,Artem Burchanow,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelectInfer是一种面向边缘设备的LLM推理优化框架，通过离线分析识别任务特定和通用神经元，实现选择性神经元加载和动态计算，在降低内存和计算开销的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临高计算和内存需求挑战，现有粗粒度剪枝或量化方法会损害精度或需要重新训练/微调，亟需更高效的优化方案。

Method: 提出SelectInfer框架，包含两个核心优化：(1) 选择性加载（selective loading）——通过离线LLM分析器识别重要神经元，推理时仅加载关键神经元子集；(2) 选择性计算（selective computation）——运行时仅动态计算最相关的神经元。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，SelectInfer在显著降低内存占用和计算量的同时，保持了任务性能。

Conclusion: SelectInfer为在边缘设备上部署LLM提供了一种实用且有效的方案，通过神经元级的细粒度优化平衡了效率与性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks, but their high computational and memory demands pose significant challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. Existing approaches to model compression and optimization often rely on coarse-grained pruning or quantization, which can compromise accuracy or require re-training and fine-tuning. In this work, we introduce SelectInfer, a neuron-level optimization framework that enables efficient LLM inference on edge devices through selective neuron loading and computation. By profiling and identifying both task-specific and general-purpose neurons using an offline LLM profiler, SelectInfer implements two key optimizations: selective loading, which reduces memory footprint by selectively loading a subset of neurons that were identified to be most important during the offline stage, and selective computation, which dynamically computes only the most relevant neurons at runtime. Evaluation across multiple datasets shows that SelectInfer achieves significant reductions in memory footprint and computation while preserving task performance, making it a practical step towards enabling LLM deployment on edge devices

</details>


### [107] [LLM-as-a-Coach: Experiential Learning for Non-Verifiable Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18110)
*Tianzhu Ye,Li Dong,Guanheng Chen,He Zhu,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出体验式学习（EL），将LLM评判模型的标量奖励反馈转化为高带宽的体验知识，通过上下文蒸馏实现策略学习，在开放任务上优于传统基于规则模板的强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL将基于规则模板的评估压缩为标量奖励，丢弃了丰富的文本反馈，且无法区分不同质量水平的回答，导致反馈信号带宽不足。

Method: 将LLM-as-a-Judge的反馈模型重用于LLM-as-a-Coach，教练模型将每个on-policy回答的评估结果提炼为可迁移的体验知识，用于条件化教师模型，并通过on-policy上下文蒸馏将知识内化到策略中。

Result: 在两种策略族上，无论使用策略自身反馈还是专有模型反馈，EL在保留和未见过的开放任务上均持续优于基于规则模板的RL；EL在分布外泛化更好，并能缓解奖励破解问题。

Conclusion: 体验知识是非可验证任务后训练中比标量奖励更丰富、泛化性更强的学习信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) on open-ended tasks compresses an LLM's rubric-based evaluation into a scalar reward, discarding rich textual feedback and conflating responses with distinct quality profiles. We propose Experiential Learning (EL), which repurposes the feedback model from an LLM-as-a-Judge into an LLM-as-a-Coach. The coach distills its assessment of each on-policy response into transferable experiential knowledge, which conditions a teacher model and is internalized by the policy through on-policy context distillation. Compared with scalar rewards, this higher-bandwidth feedback channel provides dense supervision and preserves fine-grained preferences among high-quality responses. Across two policy families, with feedback from the policy itself or a proprietary model, EL consistently outperforms rubric-based RL on held-out and unseen open-ended tasks. Notably, EL generalizes better beyond the training distribution, and mitigates reward hacking. These findings establish experiential knowledge as a richer and more generalizable learning signal for post-training on non-verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Do Language Models Dream of Binding Molecules? Benchmarking LLMs under Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18144)
*Thomas MacDougall,Maksim Kuznetsov,Roman Schutski,Rim Shayakhmetov,Maxim Malkov,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用大语言模型在基于结构的药物设计中处理3D空间约束的能力，引入了3D-Fit基准测试策略，发现LLM虽落后于专用扩散模型，但能同时处理多种空间约束，具有可扩展潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在分子设计中展现出竞争力，但其对物理和3D空间环境的推理能力尚未被充分探索，需要系统评估其与扩散模型等基线方法在复杂3D约束下的表现差距。

Method: 提出3D-Fit基准测试策略，在蛋白质口袋条件下进行3D配体生成，并引入锚定片段、药效团点和口袋-配体相互作用等多种空间约束，系统比较LLM与专用扩散模型的表现。

Result: LLM在空间分子生成上仍落后于最先进方法，但展现出处理多种空间约束的潜力，可扩展到异构设置。

Conclusion: 当前通用LLM在3D空间推理上不及专用扩散模型，但其多约束处理能力提示未来在多条件分子生成中具有应用前景。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) leverages the 3D structure of protein targets, often complemented by other spatial constraints, to generate candidate binding molecules. While diffusion models have dominated as a leading paradigm for high-quality 3D molecule generation, LLM-based methods are rapidly emerging in molecular design and have shown competitive performance in pocket-conditioned molecular generation. However, their ability to reason about physics and 3D spatial environments is largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically analyze whether current general-purpose LLMs are capable of navigating complex 3D constraints compared to established baselines such as specialized diffusion models. We consider 3D ligand generation conditioned on protein pockets together with ligand- and interaction-derived spatial constraints, including anchor fragments, pharmacophore points, and mandatory pocket-ligand interactions. To enable this evaluation, we introduce 3D-Fit - a token-efficient benchmarking strategy for assessing LLM performance on multi-conditioned spatial molecule generation. Our findings reveal a clear pattern in LLM spatial capabilities: while they still lag behind state-of-the-art approaches, they are promising and can handle multiple spatial constraints simultaneously, enabling scaling to heterogeneous setups.

</details>


### [109] [OR Else: A Differentiable Trust Region for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18163)
*Chinmay Rane,Kanishka Tyagi,Michael Manry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出Output Reset (OR)作为裁剪替代目标的平滑替代方案，用于LLM后训练。在GAE下，PPO-OR比PPO-clip平均奖励模型得分高0.305；在组间相对优势下，GRPO-OR未提高平均分，但减少了方差。两种设置下OR都改变了优化行为，但奖励效果不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO和GRPO使用的裁剪替代目标存在有利方向饱和问题，导致标量目标导数突变。作者探索Output Reset (OR)这一平滑单侧饱和规则是否能作为大语言模型后训练的有效替代方案。

Method: PPO-OR和GRPO-OR用OR平方间隔损失替代裁剪策略项，在rollout-relative token log-ratio空间中操作；优势符号决定更新方向，token在越过有利间隔后贡献零直接OR残差。使用Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct在Anthropic hh-rlhf数据集上，共享奖励模型，每种方法三个种子进行实验。

Result: GAE下PPO-OR比PPO-clip平均最终奖励模型得分高0.305，但跨种子方差更大。组间相对优势下GRPO-OR未提高平均分，但方差更小，终端OR残差接近零，超调分数下降，而GRPO裁剪目标轨迹仍可变。两种组间相对方法比GAE方法显示出更大的rollout-to-current log-ratio位移，OR未持续减少该位移。

Conclusion: OR在两种对比中改变了优化行为，但奖励效果不同。G=2时GRPO-OR诊断结果未转化为奖励分数增益，更大组数是否改变结果仍待探索。报告分数为训练时奖励模型测量值，非留出的人类偏好性能。

Abstract: PPO and the GRPO baseline studied here use clipped surrogate objectives whose favorable-direction saturation introduces an abrupt change in the scalar objective's derivative. We ask whether Output Reset (OR), a smooth one-sided saturation rule, offers a useful alternative for large language model post-training. PPO-OR and GRPO-OR replace the clipped policy term with an OR squared-margin loss in rollout-relative token log-ratio space; the advantage sign determines the update direction, and a token contributes zero direct OR residual after crossing the favorable margin. We compare PPO-clip with PPO-OR under generalized advantage estimation (GAE), and GRPO with GRPO-OR under group-relative advantages, using \texttt{Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct} on Anthropic \texttt{hh-rlhf} with one shared reward model and three seeds per method. Under GAE, PPO-OR has a mean final training-time reward-model score $0.305$ higher than PPO-clip, with a larger observed across-seed spread. Under group-relative advantages, GRPO-OR does not have a higher mean score, but shows a smaller observed spread, a near-zero terminal OR residual, and a declining overshoot fraction, while the matched GRPO clipped-objective trace remains variable. Both group-relative methods exhibit substantially larger rollout-to-current log-ratio displacement than the GAE methods, and OR does not consistently reduce it. Thus, OR changes optimization behavior in both matched comparisons, but the observed reward effect differs between them. At $G=2$, the GRPO-OR diagnostics do not translate into a reward-score gain. Whether larger groups change this outcome remains open. The reported scores are training-time reward-model measurements, not held-out human-preference performance.

</details>
